# Companion and life spells — proposed video game rules

**Pending exact authored acceptance.** Ten existing spell identities retain their full source and class access. Each full procedure below is selected from Current05 after the actual GPT conference. Two Command Undeath cases need a separately reviewed exact CR 2 undead profile.

## Shared procedures

### CL10-CAST-01

#### id

CL10-CAST-01

#### title

Source identity, access, costs and real clocks

#### classification

INHERITED interfaces with explicitly identified spell-local completions

#### scope

These ten ordinary spells; no source/catalog rewrite

#### completeText

Keep each complete current source/access object and every original Core/catalog entry unchanged. The catalog's actual class-school placement, rank and minimum class level must be paired with a real learned/prepared repertoire. A source-only access row does not supply the rest of an unsupplied class engine. No union of another class's schools is allowed. Universal Create Homunculus remains Universal; Necromancy remains Necromancy. No named material, blood, corpse, gem or monetary component is inferred from a title. Golem and Danse retain V/S/M-F evidence; the inherited focus alternative is usable without inventing consumed materials.

All ten have an Action casting time and printed 60-foot range. Preserve that range as evidence even where Soulburn's proposed self-only recovery narrows its operation. Base RP is 7 at rank 4, 10 at rank 5 and 13 at rank 6. Apply only actual accepted price rules and acquired class exceptions. Quote and pay before ordinary counter/interrupt resolution; a future recovered pool cannot fund that quotation. Source-specific HP costs are real normal-HP costs, not damage, healing, temporary HP or a shareable packet. They cannot lower the payer below 1 HP. Countered or paid hidden-ineligible outcomes do not refund HP/RP or restore displaced concentration.

Use the accepted spell-commitment, Dissonance/Refrain and Ready rules, not a generic new sequence. The acquired Wizard matrix exception can change base Dissonance; it does not erase Refrain. Idle turns do not reset either. A readied spell pays/prepares and begins held concentration at preparation; its legal target/area is selected and checked at release. The particular effect's lifetime begins only at successful release/establishment. A separately source-authorized nonspell Action repair/readied technique has its own payment at actual execution, not an invented spell cast.

NEW spell-local durations retained for authoring: Corpse Golem, Death Knight and Soul Wraith require concentration and end 60 actual seconds after establishment. Flood/Danse animation bodies end 3,600 actual seconds after their successful formation and require no concentration. Their printed Instantaneous metadata describes establishment, not a rewrite of the original. Create Undead's physical conversion is permanent; its separate rank-six authority relation and Command Undeath last until their real removal. Homunculus's created body/control is persistent with the distinct endings stated below. Soulburn and Grave Gift resolve instantaneous components and create no upkeep duration. An outside-mode six-second cycle is a clock mapping, never an extra action. Absolute deadlines, true regular owner markers, body-source expiry and already-spent command resources never reset on suppression, healing, release, renaming or mode changes.

### CL10-COMMAND-01

#### id

CL10-COMMAND-01

#### title

Created companions, ownership and stat provenance

#### classification

INHERITED command/stat rules; NEW named asset caps and creation-round policies

#### scope

Golem, Knight, Wraith, Flood/Danse zombies, Create Undead and Homunculus only

#### completeText

Keep original creator, current controller, body identity and source application as distinct records. Original creator-derived spell attacks, DCs and saves use the actual original creating class record; a temporary controller does not lend a better casting modifier. A profile that says PB as the total save bonus does not also add its ability modifier. Profile attacks add no ability damage unless that particular attack prints it. Critical hits double eligible attack dice, not flat modifiers or a later independent saving-throw aura.

The acquired Wizard Conjuration creation bonus is tested against an actual creature created by that Wizard's spell, not against the creating spell's school label alone. Snapshot its +Wizard-level maximum HP at genuine qualifying creation. The separately acquired Flesh Ascendant bonus is the current eligible controlling Wizard's +2 per Wizard level where its actual controlled-Undead predicate passes. Preserve the accepted exception that permits those two distinct bonuses together and the single strongest eligible application of each named source; no alias duplication. Later maximum-HP changes use the accepted clamp/capacity rules and do not themselves heal. If the source-owner later dies, its extant class/stat record does not become zero or a different controller's statistics.

While creator-controlled, each capable named asset resolves only in the actual attached phase after the whole owner's regular turn. A paid Bonus Action on that regular turn directs at most one eligible asset. It uses the accepted owner-wide one-command allowance and, for offense, the one-offensive-asset allowance for that numbered round across pets, minions, devices and these spells. Do not grant an independent Reaction, an extra owner turn, a second command during an extra Action or a second offensive asset because spell/class asset caps differ. The commanded asset must satisfy its own position, perception, path and resource prerequisites when it resolves. An issued command is not refunded or replayed if that phase lacks a legal action. Lack of a valid command permits only the source's actual defensive movement/Dodge, not an attack, skilled task or resource use.

NEW source limits: one Corpse Golem, one Death Knight, one Soul Wraith and one Homunculus per original caster; one Flood body; one Danse cohort containing at most five; two Create Undead authority slots. These are separate from Wizard's three standard-minion slots, Ranger Beast, Mage elite slots, Mirror assets and the existing Expedition roster. They do not multiply the shared command/offense allowances. A successful replacement ends/replaces only that spell's indicated source/body/control record. A known cap replacement must be declared before commitment; a failed new establishment never selects a substitute, but starting new concentration has already ended an incompatible prior concentration.

Golem, Knight, Wraith and Homunculus may use a genuine still-available creation-turn Bonus Action command and subsequent real attached phase; creation itself supplies no attack/task. NEW Flood, Danse and Create Undead forbid all voluntary acts in the numbered round of creation, including a bonus Dodge; outside initiative their first possible phase is the next genuine six-second cycle. The actual source lifespan starts at formation even while this prohibition applies. Creator incapacity alone does not retroactively cancel an already valid nonconcentration order; actual concentration ending or source/body loss does. An absent original owner supplies no fictitious attached phase. Command Undeath and release of a permanent Create Undead body use CL10-ORDER-01, not an invented owner-turn refresh.

### CL10-BODY-01

#### id

CL10-BODY-01

#### title

Bodies, geometry, stock and incomplete facts

#### classification

INHERITED body/gear rules; explicit scope boundaries

#### scope

Only the named authored bodies; not a world physiology or environment engine

#### completeText

Use Tiny 2.5-by-2.5-foot, Small/Medium 5-by-5-foot and Large 10-by-10-foot occupied horizontal spaces under the accepted body rule. A source body's measured height, current orientation, actual missing parts, body mass and supports are separate facts. No named class or ordinary Small/Medium Beast gets a two-foot combat body from [1,1,1]. A new scenario must print its actual body extents; numeric damage-only examples do not prove range, cone, collision, reach or close-quarters eligibility.

A created body's initial space must already fit, be unoccupied, be perceived/targetable within the source's real range and have the source-required support before casting. No future growth or creation expands the world to legalize a target. A corpse conversion occurs at its same actual physical anchor using current matter, not a free relocation to a convenient empty square. Voluntary size changes obey actual preflight. Mandatory source ending, death, reversion, body removal and release of melded cargo always happen; no body-reservation ward or whole external-event veto is created. Where this proposal explicitly calls canonical normalization, use same anchor, legal squeeze, then nearest legal adjacent space in the same unobstructed region, minimum displacement/greatest overlap/fixed neutral board tie, with no farther search or wall/occupant passage. Normalization spends no movement and creates no opportunity/charge/movement rider. Banishment's separately named conflict semantics are not imported.

All carried/worn items are real separate IDs. Apply actual physical-capacity AND Load limits, hand/slot/fit restrictions and Burdened penalties. A listed local smaller cargo limit adds a restriction; it does not cancel weight or Load. Genuine body parcels count as bodies under their source, never bundled equipment. Created intrinsic swords/wrappings/appearance have no independent harvestable stock. On actual body ending, real gear uses current-position legal item placement, nearest legal adjacent item placement or pending-placement state. No item jumps to a distant historical last-open square, and disappearance never recreates old cargo, repairs its condition or refills fuel.

Corpse-derived animations retain one consumed physical allocation, original body history, missing limbs, harvested parts, real gear and documented physiological/terrain requirements. Their fresh listed base HP is a new animation HP pool, not limb repair, Wound treatment, resurrection or corpse replenishment. No-breath/no-food/no-water/no-sleep, immunity, natural origin and soul status cannot be inferred from the Undead/Construct label. A permanent-body work/exposure scenario must bind the actual retained needs and applicable recovery rules; an unavailable need/profile fact remains an explicit input prerequisite, not an immunity. This conference gives no global corpse-decay or living-physiology engine. The newly generated finite manifestations and Homunculus likewise gain only their stated traits; source-dependent environmental use needs the actual required trait record.

Command Undeath's required CR is an authoritative mechanical profile field, not an appearance check or a derived result from these new HP values. All four Create Undead alternatives remain unrated. The complete frozen bestiary is a source-only record collection; no exact operative CR2 Undead is supplied. A positive Command fixture therefore remains conditional until a complete individually authorized eligible profile is bound.

### CL10-NULL-LIFE-01

#### id

CL10-NULL-LIFE-01

#### title

Damage roots, disabled bodies and valid healing

#### classification

INHERITED root/life order plus NEW limited animation lifecycle binding

#### scope

The seven created/animated-body spells here; no universal Undead healing grant

#### completeText

A creature attack creates its damage root at the actual accepted damage checkpoint, with that acting companion as dealer and the original creator retained separately as stat/source owner. Creator Veil does not automatically make a Golem/Knight/Wraith/zombie attack Null. Flood's ordinary spell packet has its caster as dealer. Separate attack events create separate roots; child packets inherit their parent flag; sharing preserves flags and never double-counts the parent and terminal branches. Apply the actual real damage, prevention, temporary HP, Wound/death and actual immediate-response sequence. Do not decide actual death just from reaching zero.

Qualifying Null damage uses the accepted ordered protection, including damage-caused Instant Death/Wound/Maiming/fatal-Wound exclusions and retained prior zero-state histories. It is not an HP-cost prevention, independent expiry immunity, resurrection or concentration immunity. Golem, Knight, Wraith and Homunculus bind their existing finite-created/Construct recipient branches: a qualifying Null packet taking the surviving body to zero, or hitting the same already-disabled body, records/preserves NullDisabled0 rather than ordinary damage-caused disappearance. This adds no living-ally target predicate, Wounds, death saves, Stable roll or rest regeneration to these HP-only profiles. Their own concentration, expiry, dismissal, replacement and suppression endings still happen.

NEW named extension: Flood zombies, Danse zombies and the four Create Undead bodies use the same finite or persistent consumed-animation principle for qualifying Null damage: preserve the same body/allocation, authority, HP0, gear and clocks in NullDisabled0; no fresh corpse destruction or resource trigger. This specifically binds these new profiles and does not relabel them as Wizard standard minions. Further qualifying Null damage neither destroys nor refreshes them. Independent authority/source ending, genuine non-Null damage causing the profile's ordinary zero/destroyed branch, or another actually applicable real ending remains effective. A living target of Flood instead uses its own real living/death-save/Beast lifecycle; Null preventing its death prevents Flood's zombie claim.

Legal positive restoration to the same extant disabled body clears only that zero-HP disabled state. It never refills a command, lifespan, ability, limb or inventory. Homunculus's explicit maintenance and Wraith's own self-lifetap are named permissions. Any other proposed repair/heal must supply an actual eligible target source. Cleric Healing Rite requires a living ally, and the scoped AC4/AC5/AC6 living-ally extensions do not include these new bodies. This conference does not extend that predicate. A source with its own creature-only healing permission can be evaluated on its actual terms; Undead/Construct alone supplies neither immunity nor universal eligibility.

### CL10-SUPPRESSION-01

#### id

CL10-SUPPRESSION-01

#### title

Distinct suppression and terminal identity

#### classification

NEW source-local lifecycle choices constrained by accepted antimagic and life rules

#### scope

Only these named applications under the actual suppressing source; not a global field-size rule

#### completeText

Do not unify the bodies merely because they are summons. A full operational body-expression suppression ends Corpse Golem, Soul Wraith and Homunculus immediately with current-position real cargo settlement and no stored return. For Golem/Wraith this also ends that now-empty creating application; it does not resume when the field ends. A narrower suppressed attack/rider suppresses only its own expression unless the actual field also suppresses the body sustaining expression.

Death Knight instead temporarily has no expressed body. Record its actual current pose when suppression starts, same identity/HP/NullDisabled state/current maximum/source owner/spent allowances and absolute deadline. Real external gear drops there under existing item placement, and intrinsic shadow equipment produces nothing. The absent body provides no occupancy, cover, support, attack or sensory presence. When all applicable suppressors end while the source remains live, attempt to re-express that same body at the saved pose using the local canonical fitting procedure. An occupied saved pose is not a protected berth. If no local placement is legal, expression remains pending within the original source lifetime; no extra duration is granted. Re-expression does not automatically reclaim dropped gear. A suppressed field can still contain the pending pose; it cannot resume merely by asking for an outside square. If source/clock ends first, nothing is re-created. A legal unsupported re-expression starts the actual Falling rules.

Flood and Danse keep their actual consumed physical bodies and real carried gear in place while their sustaining animation is suppressed. Suppressed animation supplies no voluntary movement, offense or command execution; gravity, ordinary collision/support, externally inflicted effects and absolute clocks remain real. Their eventual hour/zero/dispel/dismissal ending leaves the same consumed remains, not a fresh corpse. Create Undead is different again: permanent physical conversion is not undone by dispelling/suppressing its separate authority relation. Suppression of authority restores the body's underlying real independent agency; it does not grant an extra opportunity or free loyalty. Any independently magical native action/rider remains subject to the actual field on its own terms. Command Undeath similarly suppresses control, not an existing body's physical existence.

NEW limited geometric completion: when accepted Lesser Antimagic intersects any part of the actual expressed body of Golem, Knight, Wraith, Homunculus, Flood zombie or Danse zombie, treat that named body's sustaining expression as fully suppressed for the lifecycle above. This extends no other spell or field and grants no immunity to merely partial-body intersections. For a temporarily absent Knight test the saved expression volume. Local suppression of Create Undead/Command Undeath authority is tested at the actual bound body's volume; it changes authority, not permanent flesh. These specific choices are proposed here, not inherited globally from the two AC5 or three AC6 families. Use their actual source family exception and spell-disruption rules without editing accepted AC5/AC6.

A suppressed queued command is spent/lost at its one genuine opportunity, not banked for resumption. Source expiry, real body death/removal and mandatory form endings are never vetoed by suppression. Direct removal uses the actual class/ordinary Dispel rank/cost/check and immutable operation rules. A Wizard rank-three class Dispel is not an ordinary rank-three Dispel by name, and Greater Dispel does not replay a previously suppressed removal merely because a suppressing field was ended in the same captured operation set.

### CL10-ORDER-01

#### id

CL10-ORDER-01

#### title

Command Undeath and released permanent bodies

#### classification

NEW Command-Undeath/Create-Undead-only schedule and agency completion

#### scope

No extension of DE4 domination to other sources

#### completeText

Choose Q14 option B: an already independent bound creature keeps its actual pre-binding initiative position and regular opportunities. Do not migrate it to the binder's attached phase. An existing companion keeps its actual original owner's attached phase and all source-owner stats/slots. Binding, suppression, release, death of a controller and return from suppression do not create an immediate turn, refresh a used Action/Reaction, replay START/END hooks or reset a recharge. The exact actual owner or independent schedule must be bound before a current worked fixture can be claimed complete.

While Command Undeath is operative, its non-Charmed permanent restriction permits only defensive movement/Dodge without a valid command. On the binder's actual regular turn, a Bonus Action through the spell's 60-foot same-plane perceived/LoE command channel can queue one bounded order for one target's next actual future behavior opportunity. A turn already underway is not a future opportunity. The order expires at that single opportunity whether executed, suppressed, invalid, impossible or budget-starved, or at the binder's next regular START or issuance+6 actual seconds, whichever occurs first. The six-second cap is a NEW Command-Undeath-only absolute order expiry, not a new lifetime for the permanent relation. Only one pending order per binder-target exists; it cannot be banked through absence or suppression. Reaching a deadline that is also a possible execution point expires it first under the actual ordered phases. A successfully heard order does not create an ongoing remote channel: later plane separation can prevent new issue, not supply new knowledge or alter the already fixed instruction.

The issuing Bonus Action consumes the actual owner-wide command allowance of its issuance round. At execution the binder's owner-wide offensive allowance for that execution round must also be unused and is consumed once; a command that crosses a round boundary neither duplicates an offensive allowance nor refunds its prior command. For a source-owned companion, both binder's and original owner's structural allowance for that body/phase must be satisfied. Reuse an existing reservation only for the same body, same phase and same offensive opportunity; do not charge an unwilling owner a new Bonus Action or reserve another companion's attack. The absent original owner supplies no invented attached action phase. Resolve genuinely competing source-qualified directives under actual applicable ordering; this proposal does not grant authority to override another named source's explicit priority.

A valid instruction can use the body's actual movement and one ordinary Action, including a native inherent Multiattack only when the bound profile really has it. It does not force optional finite-resource powers, spellcasting, expended charges/recharges, new Bonus Actions/Reactions, secrets, prohibited oaths, item surrender or a suicide operation. Mandatory ordinary ammunition/material expenditure of an otherwise legal ordinary attack still needs the actual item; an instruction creates none. Type/obedience is not a source of private knowledge. Range and LoE for an executed attack are measured from the actual acting body, not the binder. No order changes donor or soul identity.

Suppressed Command control restores ordinary underlying agency at the same real scheduled opportunity; no instruction is performed while the control is suppressed and none replays later. When control resumes, restore the still-live relation and future defensive-only restriction without repeating its already-admitted hard-control save. Actual target death ends that relation; caster death alone does not, but a dead/absent/incapacitated binder cannot issue legal orders. The caster can release its own relation on its actual regular turn without an Action. It grants no automatic loyalty, hostility, faction membership or property ownership afterward.

For Create Undead only, losing/suppressing/replacing creator authority leaves the permanent actual body alive in its current animation state. On first independent agency, preserve that numbered round's spent opportunity and creation-round prohibition. Do not give an immediate action: place its next independent regular slot immediately after its former creator slot at that slot's next scheduled occurrence, using stable body-ID order for a group. If an old slot cannot recur because the owner has left the encounter, retain its captured virtual slot solely as a scheduling marker and assign an independent slot at the next actual encounter scheduling boundary; no retroactive turn is invented. Restoring authority retires that independent slot only prospectively into the creator's next genuinely available attached phase; at most one voluntary opportunity per round survives the transition. This is a NEW limited transition rule for these permanently created profiles, not a global controller/initiative rewrite.

Released bodies need actual NPC behavior/needs/context records before a autonomous-work/combat sequence can be claimed complete. Release is not a direction to attack, obey forever, donate HP, take a crew job or harvest another corpse. Two authority slots are not a world population cap. Repeated genuinely paid Create Undead casts with different eligible real corpses may accumulate independent bodies over time; no automatic service, material reward or free roster space follows.

Outside initiative, a released permanent body uses independent successive six-second opportunities beginning no earlier than its next unspent former cycle boundary; re-entering initiative maps that pending opportunity without replay or refresh. This defines timing only. It supplies no unreviewed NPC goal, ability, reaction budget, physiology or work permission.

## Four explicitly scoped conference choices

### CL10-SOULBURN-RESERVOIR-04

#### status

NEW_SCOPED_PROPOSAL_NOT_ADOPTED

#### scope

Only ordinary Soulburn Pact selected through Wizard Grand Reservoir

#### completeText

For this one combination, Soulburn Pact's own printed up-to-3-RP output is treated as intrinsic paid-HP conversion, not as an additional RP-refund feature layered onto Grand Reservoir. This is an explicit NEW Soulburn/Grand Reservoir interface choice; the frozen Wizard card does not name Soulburn or conclusively define this distinction. It must be separately concurred with before a positive waived-recovery case is operative. It is not a global definition of recovery/refund, a new discount, or permission to combine another refund feature.
A Wizard with the actual level-20 Grand Reservoir acquisition and its unused 1/long-rest use may select an eligible recorded Soulburn Pact. Waive only its normal 7-RP base. All actual surcharges, components, HP payment, cost commitment, cadence and interruption rules still apply. Generic Overcast, Arcane Fusion, Dual Cast and any additional RP-refund feature remain prohibited by the actual Grand Reservoir source. This exception supplies only Soulburn's own original output, not a second effect or copied casting.
Before commitment the Wizard must be able to pay every remaining real RP charge and 20 normal HP while remaining at least 1 HP. Future recovery cannot fund this preflight. At commitment pay those amounts and expend the actual Grand Reservoir use. A countered, prevented or otherwise nonresolving spell restores nothing and refunds none of those costs or the use. On successful unblocked Soulburn resolution choose an integer 0..3, limited to the missing capacity of that same real RP pool. No other resource, overflow, stored credit or additional event is created. The 0-output choice remains legal; it is not mandatory on a Reservoir cast.
With no waiver or modifier the real result remains 7 RP paid and at most 3 recovered: net -4 RP and -20 normal HP. In the isolated Wizard20 example with RP0/80 and normal HP31/122, an unused Reservoir use and no surcharge permit commitment to RP0/HP11; successful choice3 ends at RP3/HP11 with Reservoir spent. That bounded +3 output is a consequence of this expressly proposed interaction, not an unlimited loop or numerical-balance conclusion. There is still no new Soulburn rest-use cap.

### CL10-GHAST-CONE-04

#### status

NEW_SCOPED_PROPOSAL_NOT_ADOPTED

#### scope

Only the 15-foot Ghast Exhale native action in Create Undead

#### completeText

Use a finite axial circular cone measured from the Ghast's actual intact oral outlet, in a declared unit direction. This is NEW Ghast-only geometry, not inherited DI7 geometry and not a new universal cone rule. For a point p, outlet o and unit forward f, let s=(p-o) dot f and r2=|p-o|^2-s^2. The volume has 0<s<=15 feet and r2<=(s/2)^2; its terminal diameter is 15 feet. The actual outlet pose and direction are fixed for this Exhale resolution, not shifted per recipient.
A recipient must have actual body interior intersecting the cone and a clear line of effect from this same outlet to a point in that intersection. Mere touching at an apex, edge or shared exterior face is not body-interior occupancy. Resolve each actual creature once, including an ally or the Ghast if its own actual anatomy truly intersects the forward volume. This is not a perceived-enemy filter and supplies no hidden-recipient announcement, remote sense or public RNG/disclosure adapter. Solid geometry is tested on the actual segment; range and line of effect cannot use different favorable witnesses.
Retain the current profile's choice of Exhale instead of melee: one paid owner command and one shared offensive allowance. Exhale remains a magical nonspell native Action, with one shared 2d6 poison pool, actual creator DC for each CON save, full damage on failure and floored half on success. There is no lingering cloud, Poisoned condition, harvestable toxin, extra RP charge or additional attack. Missing/nonfunctional head or oral route defeats the native action's prerequisites. Current compatible Padded armor, actual AC12, missing left hand and nonproficiency remain as supplied; this CON-save native action is not a STR/DEX attack or spell cast.
In IN6-11-A, outlet (12.5,0,5) and forward +x give s=5.5 and r=1 at the two actual witnesses (18,-1,5) and (18,1,5); 1<=2.75. Both witnesses lie inside their distinct 5-by-5-by-6 bodies. The existing shared faces 3,4 total7; CON17 vs16 takes3, CON7 fails for7. No new dice or secondary rider is introduced.

## Summon Corpse Golem

| Class | School | Rank | Minimum class level | Base RP |
|---|---|---:|---:|---:|
| Cleric | Necromancy | 4 | 7 | 7 |
| Crimson Agent | Necromancy | 4 | 11 | 7 |
| Druid | Necromancy | 4 | 7 | 7 |
| Shaman | Necromancy | 4 | 10 | 7 |
| Thief | Necromancy | 4 | 18 | 7 |
| Wizard | Necromancy | 4 | 7 | 7 |

Summon Corpse Golem remains Necromancy rank 4, Action, 60 feet, base 7 RP, V/S/M-F and concentration up to one minute. Its printed HP60, 2d10 slam and slow aura all survive. NEW this is one generated temporary creature, not resurrection or assembly from actual corpse inventory. The name and material/focus alternative do not impose a corpse, soul, blood or unprinted price. Declare a perceived, supported, unoccupied Large 10x10 whole-body space within 60 feet and line of effect and bind its measured height before commitment. Pay the actual spell price and start concentration normally. Successful formation starts the same body's absolute 60-second deadline.

NEW complete bounded combat profile: Large mechanical Construct, base HP60, AC15, mass600 lb, walk20 feet, ordinary sight/hearing, no special vision. All six saves are original creating source-owner PB as the total bonus; other default untrained checks are +0. These are the inherited missing-stat fallback, not permission to invent a high attribute/proficiency. Two manipulators can carry genuinely fitting noncreature gear subject to a local maximum40 lb AND4Load, available hands and real fit. It has no fine artisan use, skill/tool proficiency, spell/resource pool, resistance, immunity, regeneration or extra natural weapon by type/name. Actual body height and needed environmental traits must be supplied in a future geometry/exposure fixture, not guessed from a two-foot box.

At its one legally commanded attached phase choose exactly one of two offensive Actions. Slam: one melee attack against an actually perceived target within 5 feet and LoE from the Golem's body, using original owner's current creating-class spell attack, dealing 2d10 bludgeoning with no ability add; attack critical uses 4d10. A true shared allowance must remain for this Golem, not another owned asset.

Slow Aura is the other offensive Action, not a free passive in addition to Slam. Establish a 10-foot radius centered on this same moving body with actual origin LoE, ending at the original source owner's next future regular START. At activation declare the finite actual enemy identities selected from opposition known to that owner/body; this supplies no hidden hostility detector or new information. Each selected creature already inside, or entering before that endpoint, makes one CON save against the actual casting DC snapshotted when the aura is activated. Success excludes that creature from this activation. Failure halves its Speed only while its body is within the live aura/LoE, under strongest-adverse-Speed arbitration. Leaving removes this aura's active modifier; re-entry restores that same failure without a second save. A later genuinely commanded activation has its own single-save record. The aura grants no Action loss, full Slow condition, hard-control claim, new attack or duplicated same-name slow. Body disappearance, incapacity where actual aura rules require consciousness, or suppression disables the aura; its original endpoint keeps running and it does not queue a later application.

There is one live Corpse Golem source per original caster. Formation does not itself attack; an available real creation-turn Bonus Action may command through CL10-COMMAND-01. Ordinary zero-HP destruction, expiry, concentration ending, actual removal or full sustaining-expression suppression ends it and the source's remaining aura, without corpse/XP/loot/harvest. Qualifying Null instead leaves the same extant body NullDisabled0 while the source persists; no dying/Wound/Stable engine is added and no generic organic-healing repair is granted. Independent ending/suppression still removes that disabled body. Real external cargo settles at the current actual location under legal item placement; intrinsic appearance creates no material stock.

The direct-recovery choice does not turn this ordinary spell into a nonspell or a blanket immunity to source-qualified anti-restoration effects. Use a finite axial circular cone measured from the Ghast's actual intact oral outlet, in a declared unit direction. This is NEW Ghast-only geometry, not inherited DI7 geometry and not a new universal cone rule. For a point p, outlet o and unit forward f, let s=(p-o) dot f and r2=|p-o|^2-s^2. A recipient must have actual body interior intersecting the cone and a clear line of effect from this same outlet to a point in that intersection. Mere touching at an apex, edge or shared exterior face is not body-interior occupancy. Resolve each actual creature once, including an ally or the Ghast if its own actual anatomy truly intersects the forward volume. This is not a perceived-enemy filter and supplies no hidden-recipient announcement, remote sense or public RNG/disclosure adapter. Solid geometry is tested on the actual segment; range and line of effect cannot use different favorable witnesses.
Retain the current profile's choice of Exhale instead of melee: one paid owner command and one shared offensive allowance. Exhale remains a magical nonspell native Action, with one shared 2d6 poison pool, actual creator DC for each CON save, full damage on failure and floored half on success. There is no lingering cloud, Poisoned condition, harvestable toxin, extra RP charge or additional attack. Missing/nonfunctional head or oral route defeats the native action's prerequisites. Current compatible Padded armor, actual AC12, missing left hand and nonproficiency remain as supplied; this CON-save native action is not a STR/DEX attack or spell cast.
In IN6-11-A, outlet (12.5,0,5) and forward +x give s=5.5 and r=1 at the two actual witnesses (18,-1,5) and (18,1,5); 1<=2.75. Both witnesses lie inside their distinct 5-by-5-by-6 bodies. The existing shared faces 3,4 total7; CON17 vs16 takes3, CON7 fails for7. No new dice or secondary rider is introduced.

## Create Homunculus

| Class | School | Rank | Minimum class level | Base RP |
|---|---|---:|---:|---:|
| Bard | Universal | 6 | 16 | 13 |
| Cleric | Universal | 6 | 11 | 13 |
| Mage | Universal | 6 | 11 | 13 |
| Mirrorsage | Universal | 6 | 16 | 13 |
| Wizard | Universal | 6 | 11 | 13 |

Create Homunculus remains Universal rank6,Action,base13RP,V/S/F,60feet and Instantaneous creation. NEW create one physical Tiny mechanical Construct in a currently perceived fitting unoccupied whole-body space within range/LoE. No blood, gem, soul, recipe, consumed material or monetary component is printed or added. Its created body and authority persist without concentration until this source's actual ending. One live body per originating caster: successful replacement ends the old body/control and settles actual carried belongings at the old body's current real location; a countered or failed replacement does not end the old body or refund a paid cast. Physical-body persistence is not a source of harvestable construct parts.

Complete proposed profile: Tiny2.5x2.5 occupied space, actual measured height, body mass8lb, baseHP20 AC14; STR6 DEX14 CON12 INT6 WIS10 CHA6; walk20, Fly30 with Hover, ordinary sight/hearing and Darkvision30. Saves use original creating class-owner currentPB as total; untrained checks use actual listed modifiers. It has small manipulating hands but no skill/tool proficiency, autonomous offense, attack, Multiattack, independent Reaction, spellcasting, class resource pool or copied caster mind. Poison-damage/Poisoned immunity is expressly NEW for this one proposed profile; no other physiology/resistance/immunity follows from Construct.

CHANGE to maximum-HP text: Universal school alone neither grants nor defeats Wizard Conjuration's actual creation bonus. If a Wizard with the genuinely acquired Conjuration feature casts this Wizard-accessible ordinary spell and actually creates this creature, the inherited +Wizard-level maximum HP snapshot applies. A Wizard11 qualifying creation therefore has base20+11=31 before any other genuinely applicable named rule. A non-Conjuration Wizard or another class does not receive it merely from the narrative. The complete current source/access row remains Universal; do not relabel the spell Conjuration or add Wizard access to another class. Flesh Ascendant's Undead predicate does not match this Construct. Later healing/repair does not recreate or refresh that snapshot.

Physical cargo cap is Tiny STR6×7.5=45lb AND3Load, subject to actual hands, fit and containers. Its own8lbbody is not its own cargo but matters to another carrier. At2Load it exceeds half of3 and is Burdened: walk20 becomes10 and Fly30 becomes20, with the actual climbing/swimming/silent-movement penalties when relevant. Combining two2Load parcels still gives4Load and is illegal; calling them one bundle does not erase contents. It cannot transport another creature through this task profile. True carried lantern fuel, damage, ownership and loading time persist.

The creator must issue real simple spoken commands through actual hearing within60feet, with owner perception/LoE and same-plane relation. No telepathy, sensory sharing, hidden knowledge or copied professional judgment is added. In combat a genuinely available Bonus Action directs one simple object-task Action and actual movement in the attached phase, sharing command allowance across assets. A real creation-turn command is allowed but casting supplies none. Fetching a reachable unattended parcel, opening an unlocked physically operable ordinary door, holding a light or sorting known mundane objects are allowed. It cannot attack, activate combat/weapon/healing/spell consumables or dangerous attack machinery, supply Help/flanking, perform skilled judgments or provide missing expertise. A capable uncommanded body only waits/moves/Dodges defensively.

Outside initiative the creator can give one clear safe unskilled standing chore through that same actual channel. The servant performs at most one mundane task interaction per6elapsedseconds plus movement at its actual current Speed, only while the real60-foot supervised relation remains. A new chore requires a new genuine instruction. When supervision/channel ceases it finishes no new unsupervised productive step and waits/defends; there is no remote shop or automatic work Watch. The saved owner's manipulation time does not create crafted output, skill/proficiency, credential, trained assistant, crew attendance, wages, simultaneous job or Expedition office. Apply each actual EX58/crafting task's own real roles and clocks. Actual independent credentials would need their own separate source and are not manufactured by this spell.

NEW maintenance: original creator, one actual Action, touch,2actualRP restores2d6 normalHP to the same still-existing damaged Homunculus up to effective maximum. This is a paid nonspell maintenance operation, not a new spell cast, Counterspell target, Refrain reset, ordinary organic Healing Rite or destroyed-body reconstruction. The2RP is actually paid under this operation, not a spell base price for Nexus/Grand Reservoir or a generic cost waiver; only an exact source expressly applying to this operation could change it. Its2d6 are real repair dice but gain no new presentation/Fate entry here. Broad gain/repair restrictions and actual eligible modifiers still apply; do not assume an organic-healing modifier targets repair. Other repairs need their own exact Construct/body eligibility. Mending explicitly does not refill this HP ledger.

At ordinary0HP destruction it ends without corpse/XP/loot/salvage. Qualifying Null instead leaves the same body NullDisabled0; the explicit maintenance may restore that extant damaged body at0, but can never repair an actually ended one. Full sustaining-source suppression, actual eligible rank-six removal, a successful replacement or creator dismissal ends it and settles real cargo at its actual position. Dismissal is this spell's actual creator Action with perception within60feet/LoE, not the other summons' no-Action concentration release. Caster death/incapacity alone does not invent an ending; absent lawful owner phase/channel there are no new commands. No repair, return, temporary controller or appearance change renews source identity, tasks, HP pool, cargo or permissions.

### completeSubmittedProcedureUnchanged

Summon Corpse Golem remains Necromancy rank 4, Action, 60 feet, base 7 RP, V/S/M-F and concentration up to one minute. Its printed HP60, 2d10 slam and slow aura all survive. NEW this is one generated temporary creature, not resurrection or assembly from actual corpse inventory. The name and material/focus alternative do not impose a corpse, soul, blood or unprinted price. Declare a perceived, supported, unoccupied Large 10x10 whole-body space within 60 feet and line of effect and bind its measured height before commitment. Pay the actual spell price and start concentration normally. Successful formation starts the same body's absolute 60-second deadline.

NEW complete bounded combat profile: Large mechanical Construct, base HP60, AC15, mass600 lb, walk20 feet, ordinary sight/hearing, no special vision. All six saves are original creating source-owner PB as the total bonus; other default untrained checks are +0. These are the inherited missing-stat fallback, not permission to invent a high attribute/proficiency. Two manipulators can carry genuinely fitting noncreature gear subject to a local maximum40 lb AND4Load, available hands and real fit. It has no fine artisan use, skill/tool proficiency, spell/resource pool, resistance, immunity, regeneration or extra natural weapon by type/name. Actual body height and needed environmental traits must be supplied in a future geometry/exposure fixture, not guessed from a two-foot box.

At its one legally commanded attached phase choose exactly one of two offensive Actions. Slam: one melee attack against an actually perceived target within 5 feet and LoE from the Golem's body, using original owner's current creating-class spell attack, dealing 2d10 bludgeoning with no ability add; attack critical uses 4d10. A true shared allowance must remain for this Golem, not another owned asset.

Slow Aura is the other offensive Action, not a free passive in addition to Slam. Establish a 10-foot radius centered on this same moving body with actual origin LoE, ending at the original source owner's next future regular START. At activation declare the finite actual enemy identities selected from opposition known to that owner/body; this supplies no hidden hostility detector or new information. Each selected creature already inside, or entering before that endpoint, makes one CON save against the actual casting DC snapshotted when the aura is activated. Success excludes that creature from this activation. Failure halves its Speed only while its body is within the live aura/LoE, under strongest-adverse-Speed arbitration. Leaving removes this aura's active modifier; re-entry restores that same failure without a second save. A later genuinely commanded activation has its own single-save record. The aura grants no Action loss, full Slow condition, hard-control claim, new attack or duplicated same-name slow. Body disappearance, incapacity where actual aura rules require consciousness, or suppression disables the aura; its original endpoint keeps running and it does not queue a later application.

There is one live Corpse Golem source per original caster. Formation does not itself attack; an available real creation-turn Bonus Action may command through CL10-COMMAND-01. Ordinary zero-HP destruction, expiry, concentration ending, actual removal or full sustaining-expression suppression ends it and the source's remaining aura, without corpse/XP/loot/harvest. Qualifying Null instead leaves the same extant body NullDisabled0 while the source persists; no dying/Wound/Stable engine is added and no generic organic-healing repair is granted. Independent ending/suppression still removes that disabled body. Real external cargo settles at the current actual location under legal item placement; intrinsic appearance creates no material stock.

### completeHistoricalRecordUnchanged

#### spellId

summon-corpse-golem

#### procedureScope

complete-proposed-mechanical-spell

#### procedure

Action; range 60 feet; V/S/M/F, using an actual unpriced material-or-focus alternative; KEEP concentration up to one minute and HP 60, 2d10 slam and slow aura. NEW create one Large construct companion in an unoccupied supported ten-foot space perceived within range and line of effect. It is a temporary summoned body with corpse-like appearance, not an animation of an actual dead identity; no corpse input, ownership or soul fact is inferred. Base AC 15, normal HP 60, walk 20 feet, body mass 600 pounds, ordinary vision/hearing, saves current original-owner PB, other checks +0. It understands owner commands but has no speech/telepathy; no unstated construct immunities. It has two manipulators for its slam and carrying at most forty pounds/four Load of fitting incidental cargo, no tool proficiency, fine crafting, armor or weapon proficiency. A legal commanded Slam is one melee spell attack using the original casting source’s current modifier, reach five feet, hit 2d10 bludgeoning with no ability modifier; natural critical doubles only the dice to 4d10. NEW Slow Aura is an alternative commanded offensive Action instead of Slam. It opens a ten-foot-radius field centered on the golem until the owner’s next future regular START, or earlier golem/source ending. Declare the owner’s ordinary known opposition set on activation; this is not an alignment/hidden-intent detector. Eligible enemies with golem-to-target line of effect at activation or first later entry make CON against the cast’s snapshotted DC once for that activation. Failure halves Speed only while actually inside that active aura with line of effect; success ignores that activation. Leaving suppresses the failed-save slowdown, reentering the same activation restores it without another save. A creature cannot acquire two half-Speed multipliers from overlapping golems; use actual strongest adverse-Speed arbitration before later systemic multipliers. No AC/DEX penalty, full-Action ceiling, Reaction denial, Prone, hard-control claim or ordinary Slow mode is imported. Activation spends the shared offensive allowance even if all save; its finite later entry checks need no second command and grant no new companion action. A fresh activation requires a later genuinely available offensive command. Without it the golem has no automatically active aura. Use the actual attached phase; its creation grants neither a slam nor aura. At zero HP, expiry, concentration loss, qualifying removal or full creating-source suppression the body/aura end without corpse/salvage; actual cargo remains. One golem per caster and all actual control caps still apply. Healing does not renew its source or command records.

#### newChoices

NEW Large construct chassis, AC 15/Speed 20/600-pound body, focus mapping without corpse input, and explicitly commanded short slow aura as an alternative to the printed slam. This command tradeoff and finite aura need joint balance review; no profile is borrowed.

#### workedCases

IN4-07-A: The caster’s Bonus Action commands Slam and spends the shared offensive allowance. After the caster’s whole turn, the golem reaches a legal target and hits for a rolled 2d10 total of 13. It cannot also open its Slow Aura, and another owned device cannot use another offensive companion Action that numbered round.

IN4-07-B: In the next legal round the caster commands Slow Aura instead. An enemy inside fails CON: Speed 30 becomes 15 under the adverse-Speed rule. Leaving the ten-foot region removes that field’s active slowdown; reentering before its original owner-START endpoint restores the failed-save effect, with no second save, Action loss or new control allowance.

#### questionId

IN4-07

#### originalQuestion

Preserve HP60,2d10 slam and a slow aura, Action/60ft/concentration up to one minute and Core V,S,M/F. Complete size/AC/speed/senses/saves, attack modifier/reach/type, placement, command phase and shared offense cap. Define the aura radius/eligibility/save, exact slowdown, cadence and duplicate handling; its name does not automatically import either Slow school package. Decide whether an actual corpse is required or this is a resource-ineligible summoned body, explicitly distinguishing material input from focus mapping. Preserve body/cargo history and finite ending; no free harvestable remains, creature-profile borrowing or extra action cycle.

### sourceReasonAndActualCaseEvidence

The printed3-versus5 conflict stays exact. Normal12 RP/31 HP ->5/11 ->8/11. Successive normal casts24RP/44HP ->20/24 ->16/4 cause repeat Dissonance2 and leave a third cast HP-ineligible. ### completeSubmittedProcedureUnchanged

Soulburn Pact remains Necromancy rank 4, Action, base7 RP, V/S/F, Instantaneous, with original 60-foot metadata retained. The sources conflict: catalog says sacrifice20HP to recover UP TO3RP; Core says recover5RP. This conference selects maximum3 for this proposed procedure, retaining both complete source entries unchanged. NEW this is self-only actual RP recovery. The printed range does not create an ally-transfer or target-sacrifice feature. It creates no soul token, damage packet or theological event.

Validate the caster's real class/repertoire, components, Action and actual current price before allowing commitment. Require current normal HP at least21 and sufficient actual RP for the final quoted spell price without counting future recovery. Pay that price and exactly20 current normal HP at legal commitment, preserving at least1HP. Temporary HP cannot pay. Resistance, immunity, armor, Wards, damage sharing, HP-loss prevention that does not explicitly waive this actual cost, healing transfers and a current-HP floor cannot substitute for genuine affordability. This cost is not damage: it produces no Wound/critical/death-save/damage-root/damage-derived resource trigger. An exact broad prohibition on paying/losing the required HP makes the cast unaffordable rather than canceling the sacrifice after gaining its benefit.

Use actual cast hooks, Dissonance/Refrain and actual counter timing. If countered, suppressed at manifestation or otherwise unable to complete its own effect, paid HP/RP remain spent and no recovery occurs. If successful, choose an integer recovery from0 through3, capped by the same caster's actual missing RP in the same pool at resolution. No RC, Conviction, BG, Stamina, future maximum, extra charge or another creature is credited. Apply an actual RP-recovery prohibition if present. No overflow, recovery copy, unspent credit or reset is created.

CHANGE: remove the old proposed one-use-between-beneficial-long-rest restriction entirely. That old wording remains historical only. Each later cast still needs a new real Action, components, HP affordability, actual RP quotation and class/cadence eligibility. There is no silent once/day substitute. No exact free-cost exploit has been supplied that warrants adding a new rest cap to this source. A genuine acquired feature such as Wizard Grand Reservoir may waive only the spell's base RP at its own actual frequency; it does not waive20HP, a surcharge, forbidden resource-refund combination or other eligibility. This procedure is a recovery effect, not a promise that every discounted combination yields a net gain.

With no discounts/surcharges, successful maximum recovery changes RP by -7+3=-4 and HP by-20. The unchosen Core5 alternative would still be -7+5=-2 RP and-20HP; neither is a profitable ordinary conversion. From12RP/31HP, commitment yields5RP/11HP and resolution may yield8RP/11HP. A countered result remains5RP/11HP.6RP or20normalHP is insufficient without the relevant genuine exception. These economics are an explicit consequence of source preservation, not a balance certification or an invitation to invent a cost waiver.

With no discounts/surcharges, successful maximum recovery changes RP by -7+3=-4 and HP by-20. NEW current-conference combination completion — CL10-SOULBURN-RESERVOIR-04
For this one combination, Soulburn Pact's own printed up-to-3-RP output is treated as intrinsic paid-HP conversion, not as an additional RP-refund feature layered onto Grand Reservoir. This is an explicit NEW Soulburn/Grand Reservoir interface choice; the frozen Wizard card does not name Soulburn or conclusively define this distinction. It must be separately concurred with before a positive waived-recovery case is operative. It is not a global definition of recovery/refund, a new discount, or permission to combine another refund feature.
A Wizard with the actual level-20 Grand Reservoir acquisition and its unused 1/long-rest use may select an eligible recorded Soulburn Pact. Waive only its normal 7-RP base. All actual surcharges, components, HP payment, cost commitment, cadence and interruption rules still apply. Generic Overcast, Arcane Fusion, Dual Cast and any additional RP-refund feature remain prohibited by the actual Grand Reservoir source. This exception supplies only Soulburn's own original output, not a second effect or copied casting.
Before commitment the Wizard must be able to pay every remaining real RP charge and 20 normal HP while remaining at least 1 HP. Future recovery cannot fund this preflight. At commitment pay those amounts and expend the actual Grand Reservoir use. A countered, prevented or otherwise nonresolving spell restores nothing and refunds none of those costs or the use. On successful unblocked Soulburn resolution choose an integer 0..3, limited to the missing capacity of that same real RP pool. No other resource, overflow, stored credit or additional event is created. The 0-output choice remains legal; it is not mandatory on a Reservoir cast.
With no waiver or modifier the real result remains 7 RP paid and at most 3 recovered: net -4 RP and -20 normal HP. In the isolated Wizard20 example with RP0/80 and normal HP31/122, an unused Reservoir use and no surcharge permit commitment to RP0/HP11; successful choice3 ends at RP3/HP11 with Reservoir spent. That bounded +3 output is a consequence of this expressly proposed interaction, not an unlimited loop or numerical-balance conclusion. There is still no new Soulburn rest-use cap.

### completeHistoricalRecordUnchanged

#### spellId

soulburn-pact

#### procedureScope

complete-proposed-mechanical-spell

#### procedure

Action; V/S/F; Instantaneous. PRESERVE THE SOURCE CONFLICT: current catalog says sacrifice 20 HP to recover up to 3 RP, while Core says recover 5 RP and lists range 60 feet/base cost 7 RP. PROPOSED CONFLICT CHOICE: use the current catalog’s up-to-three recovery, retain the full actual rank-4/base-seven casting price, and make the recipient the caster’s own RP pool. The old five-RP and sixty-foot fields remain beside this explicit self-only interpretation. NEW one character-wide Soulburn Pact use per actual beneficial completed long rest, shared across classes/copies and never refreshed by replacement, form change or nonbeneficial rest. Require an unused use, actual legal components/action and enough current RP for the genuinely computed casting price; anticipated recovery cannot pay it. Also require enough normal HP to pay all applicable real HP costs while remaining at least 1 HP, so this spell alone requires at least 21 current normal HP. At legal commitment atomically spend the Action, actual computed RP price, this use and exactly twenty normal HP before the counter window. The twenty is direct HP cost, not damage: temporary/form HP, resistance, immunity, HP floors, redirection and on-damage recovery cannot satisfy or discount it. An actual rule preventing that full cost makes the cast illegal; do not treat prevented payment as paid. A countered cast leaves all committed payments/use spent and recovers no RP. At successful resolution choose an integer recovery from zero through three, capped by genuinely missing RP in the same actual pool. Excess is lost; no bank, ally transfer, RC/other-pool substitution, Dissonance/Refrain reduction or recovery of the paid transaction is granted. Actual independently acquired RP-price discounts/waivers retain their written scope and can change the upfront price; they never change the twenty-HP payment or this one-use limit. Copied output cannot duplicate the recovery, and independently allowed fresh casts still need the unused character-wide use. NEW this preserves a deliberately inefficient ordinary transaction: without discounts/surcharges, paying seven then recovering three produces a net loss of four RP plus twenty HP. Its usefulness and the competing five-RP source are explicit design issues for joint review, not grounds to invent a free cast or silently promote a replacement design. No soul consumption, theology, life debt or corruption is established.

#### newChoices

PROPOSED source conflict choice: up to 3 RP and self only; original 5-RP/range-60 alternative retained. NEW pre-counter twenty-normal-HP payment with 1-HP floor, one character-wide beneficial-rest use and actual-cost-before-recovery ordering. The negative ordinary resource yield is openly flagged.

#### workedCases

IN4-10-A: With no discount/surcharge, a caster begins with 12 RP and 31 normal HP. Legal commitment leaves 5 RP and 11 HP, with the one use spent. Successful recovery of 3 leaves 8 RP. If countered, the result stays 5 RP/11 HP; no recovery finances the original seven-RP payment.

IN4-10-B: A caster has only 6 RP or exactly 20 normal HP, with no genuine cost exception. The cast cannot commit. Temporary HP does not cure either shortage. A real price waiver could alter only the RP check; it still requires the twenty-HP payment, unused named use and all other eligibility.

#### questionId

IN4-10

#### originalQuestion

Preserve the literal conflict: catalog sacrifices20HP to recover UP TO3RP; Core sacrifices20HP to recover5RP. Core also lists7RP casting cost, Action/60ft/Instantaneous. Resolve recovery amount, source/recipient and payment sequence explicitly; the source arithmetic is not a license to waive the printed casting price. Define whether20HP is a cost/loss or damage, affordability at low HP, tempHP/resistance/HP-floor interaction, mandatory versus optional recovery and maximum pool. A recovery promised after resolution cannot fund preflight payment. Prevent discounts, healing relays, sacrifice prevention or refund loops from inventing RP, while preserving actual separately reviewed features. No theology or soul-consumption lore is authored.

### completeSubmittedProcedureUnchanged

Summon Death Knight remains Necromancy rank5, Action, base10RP, V/S/F, 60 feet and concentration up to one minute. Preserve the printed HP80, AC18 and2d10 shadow greatsword. NEW shadow is implemented as necrotic damage only; it does not add a second slashing packet or import an external Death Knight statblock.

Create one Medium mechanical Undead in a currently perceived unoccupied supported 5x5 whole-body space within60feet/LoE. Record actual measured height/orientation. Profile: baseHP80, AC18, mass180lb, STR18 DEX10 CON16 INT6 WIS10 CHA6, walk30, ordinary sight/hearing and darkvision60. All six saves use original source-owner currentPB as the total; untrained checks use the listed ability modifier. There are no inferred immunities, resistances, fear, spellcasting, regeneration, Multiattack or legendary actions. Genuinely qualifying creation/maxHP bonuses apply before starting HP; temporary control does not replace the original attacking class. Carry real gear only under its STR18/Medium capacity, available anatomy, Load and item fit; the shadow armor/greatsword are inseparable intrinsic manifestation, not transferable equipment stock.

One actual owner Bonus Action can command one attached-phase melee attack at5ft reach using original creating-class current spell attack,2d10 necrotic and no ability damage. A critical attack doubles only to4d10. It requires actual body-origin sight/LoE and the shared owner offense allowance. No creation attack is free; a legal unspent creation-turn command can act in the actual attached phase. Uncommanded behavior is defensive movement/Dodge under the common rules.

One Knight per caster. A successful source replacement is distinct from concentration that already ended at a new commitment. The creating source has one absolute establishment+60s deadline; legal dismissal on the creator's regular turn needs no Action. Ordinary0HP, real source expiry/concentration end, eligible removal or dismissal ends the body permanently with real cargo dropped at its actual position. Qualifying Null at0 instead keeps the same disabled body while the source exists; no dying phase, new Stable timer or generic living-ally healing predicate follows.

NEW full sustaining-source suppression temporarily removes expression of the same Knight without ending its source. Capture its actual current pose, HP, maximum, Null state, creator and used budgets at suppression—not its original summoning location. Real borrowed gear drops at that pose; the shadow weapon yields nothing. An absent body supplies no attacks, sensors, cover, support or body collision. When suppression is actually absent and source time remains, re-express at that pose using same-anchor normal space, legal squeeze and then canonical nearest legal adjacent same-region placement. If no allowed fit exists, keep expression pending without holding time or reserving terrain. Recheck as the local obstruction changes; the original60s expiry still ends the source and any pending return. Reappearance retains damage and does not reclaim dropped gear. Actual unsupported legal reappearance starts Falling. No other summon inherits this suspension design.

summon-death-knight

#### procedure

KEEP Action,60feet,concentration up to60seconds and oneHP80/AC18 body with2d10 shadow greatsword. NEW summon one Medium Undead into a perceived supported unoccupied space fitting its whole body. Its base body weight is180lb; STR18,DEX10,CON16,INT6,WIS10,CHA6; walk30; ordinary sight/hearing and darkvision60; six saves each equal original owner's current PB, other ability checks use the printed modifier without proficiency. One commanded melee attack, reach5feet, original owner's current spell-attack modifier,2d10 NECROTIC damage with no added STR or other bonus. Shadow describes the created weapon and selected necrotic profile; it is not a new damage type, slashing-plus-necrotic double packet or an outside Death Knight statblock. Normal attack critical rules apply to the two dice. Use the shared companion command/phase/offense procedure; no fear aura, spell list, mount, extra attack, special save, resistance or immunity follows from its name/type. The armor and sword are intrinsic manifestations of this profile, not separately transferable items; they cannot be stripped, sold, harvested, enchanted as free equipment or retained by a copy. One active Summon Death Knight per original owner; a successfully formed new one replaces the old after formation checks, not on a failed or countered attempt (although the old concentration may already have ended). At0HP, expiry, concentration end, actual removal or owner's voluntary dismissal during their turn without an Action, it vanishes with intrinsic gear and leaves no usable remains. Real later-supplied objects drop at its actual last position using the placement ledger. During suppression its body/attack are unavailable and real carried gear drops; retain currentHP, remaining clock and original formation location at suppression. Resume only into that same space if legal, otherwise the reviewed nearest legal adjacent same-region placement or pending conflict; no remote new placement or HP reset. It contributes no support/cover while absent and cannot take commands while suppressed. Ordinary healing and genuinely source-qualified maximum-HP modifiers use the shared rules;80 is base maximum, not a free reset after taking damage.

NEW finite Medium Undead profile, necrotic meaning of shadow, attack/saves from original source owner, one-body spell cap, intrinsic nonloot gear and explicit suppression placement. Printed80/18/2d10 and concentration remain.

#### workedCases

NE5-01-A: A source Wizard9 with attack+8 creates the legal body and later pays the one BA command. Its single commanded attack hits for2d10=13necrotic; another owned skeleton cannot also spend the same round's offensive allowance. No bonus slashing packet, fear aura or second attack is added to the13.

NE5-01-B: After losing30HP from its legal starting pool, the knight is suppressed. It vanishes and drops a real borrowed lantern at its last position; the shadow sword supplies no separate item. Unsuppression within the original minute preserves the30HP loss and requires local legal placement. Expiry leaves no corpse for Danse Macabre.

### completeSubmittedProcedureUnchanged

Grave Gift remains Necromancy rank5, Action, base10RP, V/S/F,60feet and Instantaneous. Preserve sacrificing halfHP to restore an ally fully from Dying. NEW target is one other actually willing living ally at0HP, currently Dying or Stable, perceived within60feet and LoE. A positive-HP ally, self, a corpse/actually dead body, a vanished summon or an unqualified nonliving animation is not the target. A mechanically living-ally extension from a different named spell is not imported.

Before commitment require the donor to have at least2 current normalHP. The paid half is floor(current normalHP/2), with minimum1, calculated once at commitment; at least1normalHP must remain. Pay that amount and the actual final spell RP price at commitment. This is an actual HP cost, not a damage or shared/healing-transfer packet. No temporaryHP, resistance, armor, prevention, damage-sharing redirect, Debt, Death Ward or floor supplies a free cost. A donor at1HP cannot commit. An actual later death, new target death or hidden loss of eligibility can defeat resolution after costs; nothing rewinds the paid amount or makes an actually dead recipient living.

NEW the restorative component is direct state recovery, not ordinary healing: if the same eligible recipient can gain HP at resolution, set its current normalHP to its then-current effective maximum. Preserve every Wound, Injury, Critical state, anatomical loss, disease, resource, gear record and independent condition. Clear the Dying/Stable counters and recovery deadline as required by leaving0HP and remove only zero-HP-caused Unconscious/Incapacitated. Prone remains until actually ended. The ordinary-healing Critical ceiling and ordinary-healing bonuses/reductions/copies/rolls/excess-healing conversions do not transform this direct assignment. Three Wounds therefore remain Critical even at full current effective maximum; this does not cure the Wounds or change global healing ceilings.

An actual broad 'cannot regain HP' effect, such as current Chill Touch's hit rider, blocks that assignment. NEW independently, successful eligible Grave Gift resolution stabilizes a still-Dying recipient: clear its death-save counters, leaveHP0, establish exactly one actual d4-hour Stable-recovery deadline under Core. If it was already Stable, preserve its old deadline and do not roll again. This is a Grave-Gift-only stabilization component, not a general outcome of blocked heals. Dead/ineligible or wholly countered/suppressed spells receive neither component. Being Stable0 still has the actual zero-state conditions; this is not consciousness or an immediate recovery.

Actual positive HP gained can satisfy a different existing feature only when that feature's exact event/recipient predicates truly include this kind of gain. There is no ordinary-healing event, healing dice, overheal award or automatic resource reward. The ordinary cause and private numeric recovery information stay under accepted disclosure policy; a d4 Stable clock supplies no new Fate presentation grant. The donor receives no part of the benefit.

Examples retained as obligations:31HP pays15, remains16; a0/max40 recipient with3Wounds reaches40 while retaining those Wounds if unblocked.2HP pays1 and remains1; with real Chill Touch preventing gain the Dying ally instead becomes Stable0 and receives one true deadline, without refunding1HP or10RP. Current Decay is a damage/tick source and cannot be cited merely by name as the broad blocker. A fully sourced future vector must place the real blocker and Gift at their actual phases before that blocker's own expiry.

The direct-recovery choice does not turn this ordinary spell into a nonspell or a blanket immunity to source-qualified anti-restoration effects. If a separate recipient rule tests another caster's restorative/healing spell rather than ordinary healing arithmetic, retain that source's actual classification and gate. In particular the accepted False Life curse gate is not bypassed just by naming the HP assignment direct; where it applies, run its actual optional Insight and failed/declined d2 gate privately under its existing disclosure rule. It can prevent the HP component, not this expressly separate stabilization. This is not a new False Life or DE4 disclosure rule.

#### spellId

#### procedure

KEEP Action,one ally60feet,instantaneous,transfer half HP and restore fully from dying. NEW donor must be the caster with at least2current normalHP, and recipient must be a different willing living ally at0normalHP, either dying or Stable. Neither positive-HP nor actually dead creatures qualify; self-targeting cannot recycle the payment. At actual commitment snapshot donor current normalHP and pay max(1,floor(currentHP/2)) normalHP in addition to actual10RP and other casting costs. This leaves the donor at least1 by construction. TempHP is ignored for the amount and cannot pay it. It is not damage and triggers no Wound save, concentration damage save, drain, damage sharing, Death Ward floor or damage-based benefit; actual generic HP-change observers retain only their printed scope. Countering/hidden recipient ineligibility/broad HP-gain prevention refunds neither HP nor RP. A donor with1or0HP cannot legalize the cast using Death Ward or a promised later heal. After the paid spell survives countering, recheck the recipient and donor's continued ability to resolve the cast; an intervening actual incapacity/death can prevent resolution without refund or an automatic completed transfer. NEW the benefit is direct state recovery to the recipient's current effective maximumHP, not ordinary healing of the paid number. This gives literal full recovery from the zero-HP state while preserving all Wounds, Injuries, missing anatomy, diseases, curses, Corruption, Strain, resources, concentration history and unrelated conditions. Critical's ordinary-healing ceiling does not reduce this direct assignment and existing maximum-HP reductions still define the effective maximum. A broad cannot-regain-HP effect blocks the HP assignment, but NEW the independent stabilization still makes the eligible target Stable0 and clears counters. If already Stable, preserve its existing d4-hour deadline; otherwise establish it once by the reviewed Stable procedure. No repeated failed cast resets that deadline. Ordinary healing bonus/reduction/excess-conversion arithmetic does not act on this direct recovery. Actual positive-gain source triggers still require their own explicit eligibility; the paid half is not a second healing packet to copy or a separate reserve. No resurrection, regained limb or Wound removal is implied.

#### newChoices

Resolve sequence if the donor reaches0HP,dies or is protected by Death Ward, and handle dead versus dying recipients,healing prevention and failed qualification. No restoration of RP,Strain,limbs,diseases or spell slots without source authority.

### completeSubmittedProcedureUnchanged

Negative Energy Flood remains Necromancy rank5, Action, base10RP,V/S/F,60feet,Instantaneous, with5d12necrotic and its actual death-created zombie clause. NEW choose one actually perceived creature within range/LoE. It makes a CON save against the actual casting-class DC,5d12necrotic on failure or half rounded down on success. It is one ordinary caster-dealer recipient damage root; it is not negative-energy healing of Undead and has no type-immunity waiver.

Resolve real defense, sharing, temporaryHP, normalHP and life-state events first. The zombie condition requires the original target itself to actually die from this packet, not merely reach0HP, die from a later death save, be a transfer recipient or be called a Humanoid before an actual mandatory body reversion. Run all genuinely authorized immediate survival/resurrection responses; then recheck actual corpse eligibility once. No generic new response/interrupt window is created. If its death is replaced by a Ward, Undying permission, Null, a true successful resurrection or another actual source, the claim has no corpse. Flood by itself is not a personal killing blow by a Wizard standard minion and does not create an Auto-Raise reservation.

NEW immediate animation claim after those real responses: the same original recipient must leave a surviving actual Small/Medium Humanoid corpse with usable connected torso/head, same physical anchor, and no already consumed/raised/copy/no-resource origin. Preserve missing limbs, harvested parts, body weight, existing Wounds and real gear. A transformed target whose actual resulting corpse is not eligible does not qualify. One body allocation is claimed exactly once; an actual earlier legal consumer defeats a later claim. This specific immediate claim may precede a genuinely existing later Auto-Raise checkpoint, but a competing fixture must independently prove its standard-minion trigger and checkpoint rather than invent simultaneous incompatible causes of death. The claim does not move another body, repair terrain or create a replacement corpse.

Create one CL10-ZOMBIE-01 profile at that body's same lawful physical location: baseHP20,AC10,STR12 DEX6 CON14 INT3 WIS6 CHA3; Small/Medium actual body size/mass, walk20 only with surviving supports; ordinary sight/hearing, no new darkvision/language/private memories. Six saves are original creatorPB total, other untrained checks actual ability modifiers. No damage/condition immunity, regeneration, fortitude, Wounds/death-save chassis or resource pool is granted. One melee strike using an actually surviving striking limb, reach5ft, original creator current spell attack,1d6bludgeoning no ability add; crit2d6. Real worn armor and weapons require their own actual fit/proficiency/attack permission, not a free profile upgrade.

There is one Flood animation slot per caster, separate from Danse and Wizard standard minions. A genuinely created replacement ends the old Flood animation; a failed claim leaves it. No voluntary act, including Dodge, occurs in the creation numbered round. Thereafter use one real owner command/offensive allowance and attached phase. Establish an absolute3,600-second lifetime; no concentration is added to this spell. Ordinary0HP, actual animation removal, dismissal on the creator's regular turn without Action, replacement or expiry leaves the same consumed physical remains and real gear. Owner death alone does not end this nonconcentration animation, but supplies no imaginary attached actions. Full animation suppression leaves the real body inert in place with clock/gear unchanged. Qualifying Null uses the explicit CL10-NULL-LIFE-01 consumed-animation binding instead of damage-caused destruction. Neither ending nor a later new name makes the remains fresh reusable fuel or a new donor.
