### completeHistoricalRecordUnchanged

#### spellId

negative-energy-flood

#### procedure

KEEP Action,60feet,one NEW perceived creature target,5d12necrotic and zombie creation on actual Humanoid death. Target makes CON; failure takes the full shared roll, success half(floor). No special Undead healing/buff mode exists. After the damage packet and immediate survival/death/resurrection responses resolve, attempt animation only if this spell's own original target actually died from that packet, was actually Humanoid at the qualifying death and still supplies the same eligible Small/Medium Humanoid corpse. Reaching0without actual death, later bleed/death-save death, a redirected branch killing a different creature, summoned/copied/previously raised remains, absent/destroyed corpse or actual body reversion to a non-Humanoid does not qualify. A prevention/Ward or completed immediate resurrection prevents a corpse claim. No necrotic damage minimum beyond actual qualifying death is added. NEW at that immediate post-response checkpoint, before the ordinary later Auto-Raise queue, consume the one eligible corpse and create one zombie using the full shared zombie profile in its existing body space. Recheck legal space; no nearby body substitution or remote placement. A still-eligible Auto-Raise reservation does not grant two bodies; if its later check finds this body consumed, its reserved opportunity remains spent under the reviewed rule. Competing immediate effects resolve their normal source priority and then stable application ID; recheck actual corpse state before each claim. The spell grants no Auto-Raise trigger of its own. The zombie lasts one elapsed hour from formation, no concentration; destroyed at0, ended by owner's voluntary dismissal during their turn without an Action or expiry. The owner's death/incapacity alone does not end it. One Flood zombie per owner, separate from other source slots; only a successful new creation replaces the prior Flood zombie. NEW it cannot voluntarily move/Dodge/support/attack/react during its creation numbered round; outside initiative first eligibility begins the next six-second companion cycle. Thereafter use the single shared owner command/offense/attached phase, not an independent turn. Suppression leaves the actual same body inert in place, with HP/conditions/clock preserved and no abilities; normal physical gear remains. Ending leaves the same consumed remains, never reusable raising fuel or newly minted corpse inventory. Creating the zombie is animation, not actual resurrection of the former character; no former class knowledge, loyalty or soul claim is asserted.

#### newChoices

NEW single-target CON full/half, narrowly causal actual-Humanoid death, immediate post-response single body claim, completeHP20/AC10 zombie profile, hour lifetime/one Flood slot and no creation-round acts. Existing Corpse Harvest/Auto-Raise ownership is preserved separately.

#### workedCases

NE5-07-A: A failed Humanoid target takes5d12=37. It actually dies, leaves eligible Medium remains, and no immediate resurrection succeeds. One zombie forms from that one corpse and cannot act this numbered round. Its real belt pouch is the same item; neither animation nor later ending duplicates it or creates fresh harvest stock.

NE5-07-B: A target drops to0but survives dying; no zombie forms. Likewise a Ward prevents actual death, or an immediate legal resurrection marks the body living before this claim. A later failed death save is not retroactively this spell's damage death, and a reserved Auto-Raise cannot consume a body this spell already consumed.

#### questionId

NE5-07

#### originalQuestion

Preserve5d12 necrotic and CREATION OF A ZOMBIE IF A HUMANOID DIES, Core Action/60ft/Instantaneous. Define finite targets,save/outcomes and exactly which death event qualifies: this spell's actual resolved damage versus any later death. Use one physical corpse identity and actual humanoid eligibility through transformations;0HP alone is not necessarily death. Provide a complete bounded zombie profile or an exact reviewed source binding, creation delay,placement,controller/commands,allowance,lifetime and no-command behavior. Resolve simultaneous resurrection/Auto-Raise/other corpse claims,missing or destroyed body,already-animated remains and ending without duplicating body,equipment or material stock. Do not drop the zombie clause,import a familiar-game Undead buff or invent a new soul/afterlife relationship. Any true cosmology dependency stays deferred.

### completeSubmittedProcedureUnchanged

Danse Macabre remains Necromancy rank5,Action,base10RP,60feet,V/S/M-F and Instantaneous establishment. NEW declare an ordered set of one through five actual corpse IDs, each perceived within60feet/LoE before payment. Every selected body must be a real Small/Medium Humanoid corpse no older than24 actual elapsed hours, with usable connected torso/head and surviving matter for the declared form. Preserve its actual body size, height, mass, missing limbs, Wounds, harvested parts and equipment. No scattered fragment, copy, already animated/consumed body, resource-prohibited conjured corpse or merely apparent dead creature qualifies. This age limit is Danse-only and does not create a general decay rule or Create Undead age restriction.

Pay the actual spell price and casting costs. At resolution recheck the original list in order, without a sixth substitute or a secret extra selection. Each still-eligible corpse becomes the same consumed physical allocation with the full CL10-ZOMBIE-01 profile printed under Flood: baseHP20/AC10, STR12 DEX6 CON14 INT3 WIS6 CHA3, actual body size/mass, walk20 only with supports, ordinary senses, ownerPB total saves, untrained actual ability checks, and one limb-dependent melee5ft original-creator spell attack for1d6bludgeoning without add. No profile HP initialization repairs missing anatomy or gear. A body lacking legs may animate with usable torso/head but has no walking movement; a missing striking limb blocks that limb's attack until a genuine alternative source exists. Ordinary armor fit and nonproficiency remain operative.

NEW one live Danse cohort, at most five bodies, per original caster. If at least one member of the new declared cohort actually forms, end that caster's previous Danse cohort and install the new successful subset. If none forms, keep the previous cohort. Actual claimed remains from an old ended cohort are still consumed and cannot fund the new cast. This does not change the Wizard three-standard-minion cap or grant Mage/Ranger/Mirror sources.

All successful new members share one absolute establishment+3,600s deadline and require no concentration. Each has a distinct body/source-recipient record, real position, HP and cargo. No voluntary act in the creation numbered round. In later actual attached phases one real Bonus Action can command one selected member, sharing all owner assets' command/offense allowance; the other four do not attack, react independently or receive free Help. Owner death alone does not invent a source ending or an attached phase. Each ordinary0HP/actual source removal/dismissal/member ending leaves that same consumed body's remains; one member's loss does not reset or refund the other four. Cohort dismissal and successful replacement end all remaining members at their actual locations.

Full sustaining-expression suppression leaves an affected physical body inert in place, with actual support/collision/gear and clocks continuing. Qualifying Null damage instead of ordinary destruction preserves the same NullDisabled0 consumed animation under the explicit named extension; it does not roll a Stable deadline or borrow living-ally healing. Ending the hour or source still ends that animation, disabled or not. Real restoration/resurrection must independently satisfy its own body and will prerequisites; this source offers no soul, biography, resurrection or reusable corpse claim.

### completeHistoricalRecordUnchanged

#### spellId

danse-macabre

#### procedure

KEEP Action,60feet,V/S/M/F and up to FIVE actual corpses animated as Undead. NEW choose a declared ordered list of one to five distinct perceived Small/Medium Humanoid corpses, each dead no more than24elapsed hours, not previously raised/summoned/copied/illusory/fragmented, with a substantially continuous torso and head and enough remaining body to occupy its real native space. The24-hour admission is a NEW spell limit, not global corpse decay. Missing limbs, prior Wounds/Injuries, structural destruction, inventory and harvested losses remain; no severed fragment can count as another corpse or regrow a limb. No race-specific known soul, afterlife judgment or former class allegiance is required. At resolution independently recheck each chosen body, its actual corpse state and legal original space, then consume it once and animate the shared complete zombie profile. Invalid hidden entries fail without replacement, refund or a post-result sixth selection. NEW all bodies are one cohort lasting one elapsed hour, no concentration, with an original-owner cap of five Danse bodies across all casts. A successfully formed new cohort replaces all of that owner's earlier Danse bodies; if none forms the old cohort is not replaced. This cap is not the Wizard three-standard-minion cap: genuine ownership of those features and slots remains separate, and casting Danse never grants Corpse Harvest/Auto-Raise/Mage assets. All five bodies still share the owner's single BA command and one offensive companion Action per numbered round with every other companion source. A command chooses one body; it does not command five attacks. Others move defensively and Dodge only in their existing attached phase. NEW no Danse body acts during its creation numbered round; outside initiative first opportunity is next six-second cycle. Each body independently ends at0, its own actual destruction/removal, owner's voluntary dismissal on their turn without an Action, or the original common hour endpoint. One loss does not free a new body without another paid spell or reset survivors' HP. Owner incapacity/death alone does not dismiss them; suppression makes actual bodies inert in place with their real equipment and history. Animation starts the new permitted HP pool without repairing actual wounds/anatomy or restocking former gear. Native anatomy and real equipped armor follow the shared profile; no copied life resources or item attack list is acquired. Expiry/ending leaves the same consumed inert remains and actual surviving gear; no duplicate corpse, renewed component quality, health restoration of the former living person or free resurrection is granted. Each body record retains original identity, body-consumption event, wound/harvest/inventory history and current animation record.

#### newChoices

NEW Small/Medium Humanoid/24-hour/body-completeness admission, one-hour five-body cohort separate from standard-minion slots, full zombie profile, single shared command/offense and no creation-round acts. Physical original histories persist through consumption/ending.

#### workedCases

NE5-08-A: Five actual eligible bodies are selected, all within60feet. Five new animation records form, with distinct retained corpse identities and one common hour deadline. On the next eligible round the caster pays one BA to command the second zombie's one attack; the other four cannot add attacks or independent Reactions.

NE5-08-B: One selected body has already been consumed, and another lacks its legs. The first fails with no sixth-body substitution; the second can animate if its torso/head are sufficient but has no walking mode. Ending later leaves the same consumed remains and real gear. A Wizard's three separate standard-minion slots are neither erased nor expanded by the five-body spell.

#### questionId

NE5-08

#### originalQuestion

Preserve animating UP TO FIVE actual corpses as Undead, Core Action/60ft/Instantaneous. Define each eligible corpse's size,type,condition,completeness and prior identity, source of the resulting stats and equipment, finite duration/concentration and placement. Supply command cost,initiative/action limits,owner allowances,uncommanded behavior and independent loss/ending for five bodies under the creature lifecycle, without silently replacing the source count with one. Reconcile Wizard standard minions/Auto-Raise and Mage assets through their exact independent caps and body claims; being a caster of this spell does not grant either class feature. Preserve prior wounds,missing parts,inventory,harvest losses and origin restrictions rather than creating five refreshed resource-bearing bodies. State what happens after death,suppression or control expiry, and do not infer souls,planar access or cosmological resurrection.

### completeSubmittedProcedureUnchanged

Summon Soul Wraith remains Necromancy rank6,Action,base13RP,60feet,V/S/F and concentration up to one minute, with HP100/AC18, incorporeal movement and2d10necrotic plus lifetap. NEW create one Medium mechanical Undead manifestation in a currently perceived fitting unoccupied space. It is not a captured person's soul. Its horizontal occupied space is5x5; each future physical fixture must specify actual height. STR6 DEX16 CON14 INT6 WIS12 CHA8, Fly40 with Hover, no independent walk/swim/climb speed, ordinary sight/hearing and darkvision60. Six saves use original creating ownerPB total; other untrained checks use its listed ability modifier. No damage resistance, condition immunity, regeneration, possession or private memories are granted by incorporeal/Undead.

One genuine owner Bonus Action commands one attached-phase melee spell-attack-type roll from the Wraith's own actual body at reach5feet, using original creating-class current attack modifier. It is a native companion Action, not a new spell cast:2d10necrotic, no ability add,4d10 on a genuine attack critical. It spends the actual shared command/offense allowances; it grants no Multiattack, independent Reaction or free creation attack. A legally available creation-turn command can still act in the real phase.

NEW self-lifetap is this Wraith's own ordinary HP recovery, not the owner's. After this attack's actual damage and immediate death/source-ending responses, sum only eligible numeric terminal damage once: actual temporaryHP absorbed plus actual normalHP lost, never overkill, prevented/floor loss or duplicated parent damage. First apply inherited transfer exclusions: a no-derived-healing Burden Shared branch and a Life Linked no-recovery branch fund nothing. Donor classification/provenance is recorded at the actual damage event; a genuine death after that eligible damage does not retroactively erase the funding. Then apply the Wraith-only donor filter: exclude temporary summons, copies/illusions, resource-prohibited creations and consumed spell-animated bodies. Type alone is not the filter; a genuine non-created Undead/Construct creature is not excluded merely by type. Excluded branches may take damage but contribute0. This changes neither broader Vampiric Touch nor Soul Tap's different hostile-living-nonconstruct rule.

NEW current-conference body completions
The manifested Soul Wraith has two usable gripping extremities. This is a NEW physical handling completion, not a fact inferred from Undead, soul or incorporeal labels. It permits physically compatible ordinary holding, pickup and release/drop of real noncreature belongings only through the actual available interaction/Action and paid companion-command schedule. It creates no extra hand interaction, independent Action, Reaction, weapon attack, weapon/tool/armor proficiency, consumable-use privilege, professional task or general Homunculus work program. Incapacitated/NullDisabled state still prevents the relevant voluntary operations.
External cargo counts its full real weight and Load, including containers and contents once. The Wraith's actual STR6/Medium capacities are 90 lb and 6 Load; positive Load at or above 3 makes it Burdened under the accepted rule. Real cargo does not share the Wraith's wall-passage permission. Before a voluntary solid-contact segment it must have a genuinely legal external route/custody arrangement or be placed through its actual available interaction at a currently legal reachable position. No historical entry square receives teleported cargo. The chest in IN6-07-B is a prior lawful 20 lb/2 Load item, below the Burdened threshold, released into the stated open entry-floor box before traversal. The hands supply none of the chest, its contents, sight through the wall, or a new movement allowance.

Select 0 lb as the unladen physical body-weight/massLb value of this one generated manifestation. This is an express NEW profile value retained from the disclosed current fixture input, not recovered source text and not an inference about souls, native Undead, incorporeal entities or physics generally. The occupied Size remains Medium with a 5-by-5-foot footprint and the actual measured height; zero pounds never means zero occupied volume.
Its own mass does not change its STR6 carrying capacity. Real gear always retains its actual mass and Load. If another effect independently permits someone to carry this distinct creature, the accepted Medium body contributes 6 casualty Load plus all applicable carried gear Load; separately account 0 lb body plus every real item's pounds. This completion alone does not authorize picking up a creature, convert it into inventory, grant a harness, waive compatible anatomy, grant levitation to a carrier, or bypass custody limits. Any source with an actual weight-conditioned predicate evaluates that predicate as written; there is no blanket immunity to forced movement, collision, attacks, terrain, support or damage.
The printed/proposed Fly40/Hover and source-specific full-suppression ending remain separate rules. This mass choice adds neither generic no-breath status nor a new suppression/Falling exception. It is sufficient to bind the existing 0-lb Wraith inputs in IN6-07-A/B and the suppression/Null/funding cases to a stated proposed profile. New strength, transport or engineering scenarios still need their real source permissions and complete geometry.

### completeHistoricalRecordUnchanged

#### spellId

summon-soul-wraith

#### procedure

KEEP Action, 60 feet, V/S/F, concentration up to one minute, one HP 100/AC 18 incorporeal creature and 2d10 necrotic plus lifetap. NEW create one Medium spell manifestation with mechanical Undead type in a perceived unoccupied whole-body space within range/line of effect. It is not a captured named soul and grants no conclusion about afterlife or resurrection. Base HP 100 and AC 18 are fixed before actual source-qualified modifiers. STR 6, DEX 16, CON 14, INT 6, WIS 12, CHA 8; Fly 40 with Hover, no separate walk/swim/climb speed. Ordinary sight/hearing and darkvision 60. All six saves use its original owner's current PB as total bonus; other checks use its listed ability modifiers without proficiency. There are no implied damage resistances, condition immunities, regeneration, possession, spell list or private memories.

Use the shared created-companion procedure. It can receive a real BA command on the casting turn if the actual action budget and owner-wide allowances permit, then move/act only in the attached phase after that whole turn; casting creates no free immediate attack. One commanded melee spell attack at reach 5 feet uses original owner's current casting attack modifier and deals 2d10 necrotic, with no ability damage bonus. Normal attack critical doubles the damage dice only. This attack spends the shared offensive allowance and gives no Multiattack or independent Reaction.

NEW lifetap heals this same still-existing Wraith, not its owner, for floor(eligible actualDamageDealt / 2) once after the attack's damage/death responses. Sum eligible terminal branches without duplicate parent damage, overkill or HP-floor loss. Eligible funding comes only from actually damaged real creature recipients not marked as temporary summons, copies, illusions, resource-prohibited creations or spell-animated consumed bodies. Type alone, including Undead/Construct, is not a funding exclusion; actual provenance and defenses govern. Ineligible branches can still suffer the attack but contribute zero healing. Temporary HP truly absorbed on an eligible recipient counts. Apply ordinary companion healing modifiers, prohibitions and current maximum; unused healing is lost. A destroyed Wraith does not return through a queued heal. This explicit NEW funding restriction and recipient choice need joint agreement against Vampiric Touch's broader source.

NEW incorporeal movement may pass through creatures and ordinary solid objects as difficult terrain, spending 2 feet of its movement budget per foot, without granting sight or attack line of effect through them. It cannot pass an actual effect explicitly barring incorporeal passage. Its own intrinsic manifestation moves with it; real carried objects and passengers cannot pass through solid matter by borrowing this trait. While carrying one, only movement legal for that whole real load is possible. Ordinary carrying uses STR 6 and real Load; incorporeal does not grant weightless transport or more hands. It must end movement/its phase in genuinely open whole-body space. If caught inside matter by involuntary movement or an intervening change, at its phase END it takes 1d10 force once and is expelled along its recorded entry path to the last legal open point on the entry side, without advancing through the obstacle. If no recorded path exists, choose nearest legal open space, neutral stable tie order; this involuntary rescue grants no remaining movement, Action or attack. A still-impossible placement ends the manifestation. No attack can be made from within opaque matter without actual perception and line of effect.

One active Soul Wraith per caster; a genuinely formed replacement ends the old, subject to concentration commitment already ending it. At zero HP, expiry, concentration end, eligible removal or full suppression of its creating source, it ends immediately and leaves no corpse/resource or returning stored body. This NEW suppression-ending rule is specific to this manifestation. Actual carried gear drops at its last legal open position; no gear teleports forward through a wall or duplicates. No Wounds/death-save phase is created for this finite HP-only summon. Its clock, spent commands and original owner identity cannot be refreshed by renaming, healing or temporary control.

#### workedCases

IN6-07-A: A Cleric-created Wraith hits for 17 necrotic after defenses. An eligible target has 5 temporary HP and 7 ordinary HP, so eligible actualDamageDealt is 12; the 5 excess is not counted. Base lifetap healing is 6. This Wraith has actual current HP 97 and maximum 100, with no other healing modifiers, and regains only 3. The Cleric receives no healing, and the unused 3 is not stored.

IN6-07-B: Passing through 15 feet of ordinary wall costs 30 feet from Fly 40, leaving 10 feet of movement only if the Wraith can end legally. It gets no sight or attack through that wall and cannot carry a real chest through it. Full suppression ends this particular manifestation instead of storing it for reappearance; its actual cargo drops on the last legal open side.

#### newChoices

NEW full finite Wraith profile, owner-attached command, self-only provenance-qualified lifetap, measured incorporeal passage/expulsion and source-specific suppression ending.

Bind life gain to actual permitted damage quantity and named recipient/ceiling; no infinite healing from objects,phantoms or no-resource origins. Define incorporeal passage,solid endings,terrain,carrying and targeting instead of treating the word as automatic invulnerability or unlimited flight. The title supplies no claim that an actual dead person's soul was captured; do not decide soul ownership,afterlife or resurrection cosmology. ### completeSubmittedProcedureUnchanged

Command Undeath remains Necromancy rank6,Action,base13RP,V/S/F,60feet and Until Dispelled. Preserve permanently binding up to two preexisting Undead of printed CR2. Choose literal exactlyCR2, not CR2-or-less. This spell neither creates a body nor assigns CR from name, HP, level or apparent type. Before claiming a positive fully sourced case, bind an actual operative preexisting creature profile with authoritative Undead type and CR2, its actual position, body/history, original source and current schedule. The frozen source-only bestiary and these new unrated Ghoul/Ghast/Wight/Mummy profiles do not supply that prerequisite. A known ineligible target fails preflight; hidden unknown facts may defeat paid establishment without a diagnostic or replacement target.

Declare one or two actual creature IDs perceived within60feet/LoE. Pay actual spell costs; each independently makes a WIS save against the actual casting DC. A failed save can establish this NEW permanent non-Charmed whole-Action control only if the accepted source-character/target/encounter hard-control admission remains available and no exact immunity/prevention defeats it. A successful save establishes nothing. The guard is consumed on actual control establishment, not each later commanded turn; no generic repeat save, damage-ending save, concentration or short duration is added. Initial full suppression prevents control establishment under the actual spell manifestation rule.

NEW at most two live Command Undeath relations per original binder and one live relation of this spell per target. When filling full own slots, predeclare the own relation to release for each proposed new target; only actual successful new establishments release those mapped old relations. Saved/blocked/countered/hidden-ineligible targets do not free old slots. A new successful binding on an already Command-bound target replaces that spell's old control relation only after the new paid save/guard succeeds. Other differently named controls retain their own actual source arbitration, never an implied universal newest-spell override.

Control is not property transfer, a new soul/origin, created-undead identity, healing, a refreshed source, new gear or altered original-owner statistics. The actual original creature's HP, anatomy, resource uses, recharge, equipment, intrinsic restrictions and previous death/body history persist. A genuine eligible current-controller maximum-HP feature is handled under its own exception, without resetting base HP or conjuration snapshot.

CHANGE choose CL10-ORDER-01's source-local Q14B: independent bodies keep their original actual regular schedule. Existing companions keep their original owner's actual attached phase, slots and structural restrictions. A paid binder Bonus Action on a real regular turn, through same-plane perception/LoE within60feet, queues at most one bounded order for one next future actual opportunity. Never retroactively command the turn underway. It consumes the issuance-round command allowance; the real execution-round shared offensive allowance and any original companion-owner structural reservation must separately be legal. No invented unwilling-owner Bonus Action is charged. Only the same body/phase allowance may be reused once. A lost/suppressed/invalid order expires and never replays. Until validly commanded, operative binding permits only source-local defensive Move/Dodge; suppressed binding restores actual underlying agency at the same original schedule.

Orders use only actual movement and one allowed ordinary Action/native inherent Multiattack, not optional finite-resource powers, spells, extra Reactions/Bonus Actions, secrets, suicide, forbidden oath behavior or surrender of property. Mandatory ordinary ammo remains real. The source supplies neither remote senses nor additional intelligence. An already received fixed order may reach its real pending opportunity after separation, but it creates no new remote order and must meet its actual execution predicates then.

Binding lasts until actual rank-qualified removal, original caster's no-Action release on its regular turn, actual target death, or a successful replacement of this spell's relation. Caster death/incapacity or distance alone does not end permanent binding, but prevents new commands unless actual source permissions allow them. Full authority suppression suspends only this control and returns underlying agency; the body remains. Resumption keeps the original binding/guard/history without replaying old orders or granting another turn. Released creatures are not automatically allies, enemies, citizens or a free labor force. The missing exactCR2 profile is an explicit remaining content prerequisite for a future unconditional case, not a choice to leave these control rules undefined.

NEW remaining admission/channel precision: WIS is an actual initial save; this proposal supplies no automatic willing-failure shortcut. Exact source-granted save replacements/rerolls keep their own rules. Undead and exactCR2 are admission predicates, not a repeated per-turn test: an actual later anatomy/classification change does not by itself erase the still-same binding, although actual death, body removal or an explicit ending does. Orders are simple mental delivery only through this relation, within the stated same-plane60ft/perception/LoE channel; they require no new language or hearing ability, but grant no mind-reading or general telepathy. A changing body still uses its actual current anatomy, actions and original lifecycle.

### completeHistoricalRecordUnchanged

#### spellId

command-undeath

#### procedure

KEEP Action, 60 feet, V/S/F, up to two preexisting undead, printed CR 2 and permanent binding until dispelled. NEW interpret CR 2 literally as exactly CR 2, not CR 2-or-less. Declare one or two distinct perceived creatures with line of effect and genuine preexisting profiles. Actual Undead classification and documented CR 2 are checked after legal commitment when hidden; an unknown/unrated CR does not become CR 2 by appearance or by this spell. No new creature, corpse, statblock or resource pool is created. Each eligible creature makes WIS. Success establishes nothing. Failure attempts a non-Charmed whole-Action control relation under the caster/target/encounter hard-control allowance and actual defenses; admission blocked for that component establishes no binding. No repeat save, damage-ending condition or concentration is added.

NEW at most two active Command Undeath bindings per caster across all casts, and at most one such binding on a creature across casters. Before commitment designate any own old binding that a new successful target will replace if capacity is full. Only an actually established new binding releases its designated old one; failed/ineligible selections do not free slots or replace a previous controller. A new admitted binder replaces the prior Command Undeath on that recipient without duplicating its body, gear, HP, history or actions. Existing other named controls are not erased: actual mandatory orders still use their established Compelled Budget Arbitration. All hard-control histories remain spent even if a different order/controller wins.

While bound, a previously independent creature uses one attached phase after its current binder's ordinary regular turn instead of its former independent action opportunity. NEW transition rule: switching into or out of this schedule never gives a second Action/movement opportunity in a regular six-second cycle already used by that physical creature; retain its actual spent budgets and next unresolved regular opportunity. A creature already on an actual companion chassis keeps its original owner's phase, bond, source stats, body caps, upkeep and lifecycle. Binding supplies no replacement original-owner PB or ability scores. A binder command costs the binder's real BA on its regular turn, selects one bound creature perceived within 60 feet/line of effect and uses the binder's shared command/offensive allowances. For an already-owned companion it also occupies the original owner's structural command/offense allowance, reusing only a still-pending reservation for that same body/phase under the reviewed domination bridge; it never charges an unwilling original owner another BA or steals a different body's reservation.

The commanded act resolves in that creature's one real phase. Use its actual preexisting ordinary attack/native attack sequence, not a borrowed Wizard profile. Ordinary mundane ammunition is real; no spell/RP, optional finite rider, charge, consumable magic, recharge/per-rest ability, secret disclosure, permanent valuable-property surrender, knowingly binding-oath breach or suicidal order is compelled. Movement is ordinary, spends actual budgets and can provoke opportunity attacks. The relation conveys simple mental orders, not remote senses, knowledge, new languages or ownership of actual property. Without a valid available command it has only defensive movement and Dodge while the binding is operative; this is the NEW permanent control restriction whose hard admission was claimed initially. No standing order supplies automatic future attacks or out-of-combat work beyond actual commanded actions and real work/rest rules.

There is no ongoing establishment-range tether. Beyond command range the binding remains but supplies no remote commands. Caster incapacity/death leaves it dormant until a legitimate command/phase is again available; it does not become a new owner or gain an invented phase from an absent owner. Suppression temporarily releases the magical behavioral restriction and restores only its real ordinary prior chassis/agency under the no-duplicate-opportunity rule. Resumption restores the same relation with its history intact. Actual dispelling/removal, target death, caster's voluntary release during their own turn without an Action, or an admitted replacement ends the binding, never destroying the real creature or restoring spent resources. An ended relation grants no hostility, loyalty or permanent faction change; use existing character facts. Until one of those endings, the indefinite spell remains an ordinary rank-six dispellable control source, including after its caster's death. These explicit permanence, agency and scheduling choices require joint review.

#### workedCases

IN6-09-A: One actual Undead has a documented CR 2 profile; a second creature is Undead with no recorded CR. The paid cast can establish a binding only on the first after its failed WIS and available hard-control admission. The unrecorded CR is not silently treated as 2 or a successful diagnostic. Both are preexisting bodies; no HP, equipment or corpse allocation is created.

IN6-09-B: A binder already has two relations and declares which old relation a new successful binding would replace. The new target succeeds on WIS, so the old relation stays and the paid 13 RP is spent. On a later actual successful replacement, the released creature keeps its real body and spent turn budget; it does not take an immediate bonus turn. A commanded existing companion still needs its original owner's real phase and structural allowances.

#### newChoices

NEW literal CR 2 eligibility, WIS/hard admission, two-per-caster and one-per-recipient permanent control, preserved original profiles and explicit no-duplicate scheduling/dual-owner command bridge.

#### questionId

IN6-09

#### originalQuestion

Preserve PERMANENTLY BINDING UP TO2 UNDEAD OF CR2,Core Action/60ft/UNTIL DISPELLED. Resolve exactly CR2 versus CR2-or-less explicitly; do not quietly broaden the source cap or erase permanent duration. Require an actual preexisting creature profile and CR evidence,legal targeting,initial resistance/consent and maintained source identity. Define control versus ownership,commands,conflicting controllers,death/caster death,range after establishment,suppression and dispelling. Apply shared owner command/offensive allowances across all assets; repeated casts need a finite active-binding policy without inventing extra independent turns or free spellcasting. A bound undead is not newly created by this spell and keeps its real origin/resource restrictions. Treat personal agency and existing undead mechanics concretely without making new claims about soul fate,afterlife or cosmology.

### completeSubmittedProcedureUnchanged

Create Undead remains Necromancy rank6,Action,base13RP,V/S/F,60feet and Instantaneous physical creation. Preserve four distinct selectable outcomes: Ghoul, Ghast, Wight and Mummy. NEW each cast converts exactly one actual Small/Medium Humanoid corpse into one selected profile, not multiple copies or a generic zombie. No unprinted gem, soul, ingredient, costly reagent, CR assignment or imported monster statblock is added. All four explicit profile objects supplied below remain distinct and unrated.

Select the actual corpse, exact profile and any own authority slot to release before payment. Require a genuinely usable connected torso/head and enough surviving actual matter for that body, perceived within60feet/LoE at its current physical anchor. Unlike Danse, no arbitrary24-hour age cap applies; real present decay, missing parts and harvested quantities decide actual usability. Copies, fragments, vanished summons, already consumed/raised bodies and resource-prohibited artificial corpses cannot fund another physical allocation. Known failure is rejected before commitment; a hidden later failure spends the real spell costs without selecting another body. Retain all body/gear/anatomy/history and actual documented needs. New animation HP does not repair limbs, remove Wounds, add proficiencies or duplicate armor.

NEW permanent physical conversion is completed once. The consumed body persists independently of its original caster and its original corpse never becomes fresh fuel again. This is a mechanical body record, not a soul claim or forced cosmological resurrection. Its base HP is initialized once with only genuinely qualifying creation modifiers. Active source-owner/controller changes use actual MAXIMUM_HP rules, not new HP resets. Each is an HP-only animation: ordinary actual0HP destruction leaves consumed remains and real gear; qualifying Null instead preserves the same NullDisabled0 body under the explicit named animation extension, with no Wounds/death-save/Stable engine added. A later genuine resurrection must use its own actual requirements and cannot spend the same allocation twice.

NEW separate ongoing rank-six authority relation: the caster controls at most two such creations across all four alternatives. A successful third creation releases only the predeclared old authority, not that old physical body. Failed creation releases nothing. Creation's initially cooperative mechanical chassis is not a hostile preexisting target and does not claim Command Undeath's initial hard-control admission; any later hostile control must use its own actual save/guard. One actual owner command/offense per numbered round still applies across all assets, and no voluntarily acting creation appears in its creation round. While controlled, use the original owner's actual attached phase. On authority loss/suppression/reacquisition use the explicit finite, no-duplicate schedule transition in CL10-ORDER-01.

Dispelling or suppressing the authority does not undo permanent flesh, destroy the old body, refill HP or create a new corpse. Suppressed/released bodies have their actual independent agency, not automatic creator loyalty or compulsory hostility. Permanent control after release is not silently retained through old orders. A later Create Undead cannot rebind an extant living animation as a corpse. Command Undeath requires a genuine documented CR2 profile and cannot be used on these unrated designs by convenience. Actual separately valid control is evaluated on its own terms.

Explicit consequence chosen under Q15A: repeating real13-RP-or-actually-modified casts with different eligible corpses and real releases can accumulate more than two permanent independent bodies over time. Two authority slots do not impose a world population cap. None of those bodies automatically becomes an Expedition member, trained assistant, wage earner, controllable army, renewable HP donor or harvest award. Real independent needs, decisions, location and opportunities are content/state prerequisites; no video-game performance cap is smuggled into the authored rule. No granted no-breath/food/water/sleep immunity follows from Undead: retain actual recorded requirements and bind missing body/needs facts before claiming a long-term environmental or work fixture complete.

Common profile envelope: actual original Small/Medium body space and mass, measured height, real missing limbs, ordinary sight/hearing and Darkvision60. All six saves are original creating class-ownerPB total; other untrained checks use the profile ability modifier. No unprinted resistances, condition immunity, fortitude, hunger-based resource recovery, spells or class RP are created. Compatible real armor can replace base AC only under actual fitting/proficiency penalties. Intrinsic appearance/wrapping is not salvage. Physical limb attacks remain ordinary physical native attacks; NEW the Ghoul's slowing rider, Ghast's directed grave exhalation and Wight's necrotic projection are source-specific magical nonspell components, not new spell casts. Actual antimagic can suppress those expressions without undoing the permanent body; physical ordinary actions and underlying agency remain subject to their real prerequisites. Damage type alone supplies no general magical classification outside these named profiles.

Ghoul: baseHP40 AC13; STR14 DEX14 CON12 INT6 WIS10 CHA6; walk40, climb20 with surviving climbing anatomy. One melee5ft original-creator spell-attack modifier,2d6slashing from an actual striking limb. A positive post-defense/pre-temp-HP hit packet prompts CON vs original creating class current DC; failure gives Speed-10 until the target's next future regular END excluding one underway, or6elapsedseconds outside initiative. Strongest-adverse-Speed arbitration; no paralysis, hard Action denial or additive slow stacking.

Ghast: baseHP50 AC14; STR14 DEX12 CON16 INT6 WIS10 CHA6; walk30 with supports. One melee5ft2d6slashing original-creator attack OR one directed15-foot grave-exhalation cone from its actual head/oral outlet, all creatures with LoE including allies, CON vs original creator current DC for2d6poison, half on success. These are alternative uses of the same offensive Action. No persistent gas, disease, Poisoned, collectible toxin or recharge pool.

Wight: baseHP45 AC15; STR12 DEX16 CON12 INT8 WIS12 CHA8; walk30 with supports. One ranged60ft attack using original creating class current spell attack for2d8necrotic, no ability add, from actual Wight sight/LoE. Actual close-quarters/ranged conditions apply. This native magical nonspell attack is not a spell cast, physical arrow, life drain, maxHP reduction or create-undead ability.

Mummy: baseHP70 AC16; STR18 DEX6 CON16 INT6 WIS12 CHA8; walk20 with supports. One limb-dependent melee5ft original-creator attack for2d10bludgeoning without add. No fear gaze, curse, rot, disease or fire vulnerability is imported. Apparent new wrapping is inseparable cosmetic presentation, not transferable textile stock.

### completeProposedCurrentProcedure

Create Undead remains Necromancy rank6,Action,base13RP,V/S/F,60feet and Instantaneous physical creation. Preserve four distinct selectable outcomes: Ghoul, Ghast, Wight and Mummy. NEW each cast converts exactly one actual Small/Medium Humanoid corpse into one selected profile, not multiple copies or a generic zombie. No unprinted gem, soul, ingredient, costly reagent, CR assignment or imported monster statblock is added. All four explicit profile objects supplied below remain distinct and unrated.

Select the actual corpse, exact profile and any own authority slot to release before payment. Require a genuinely usable connected torso/head and enough surviving actual matter for that body, perceived within60feet/LoE at its current physical anchor. Unlike Danse, no arbitrary24-hour age cap applies; real present decay, missing parts and harvested quantities decide actual usability. Copies, fragments, vanished summons, already consumed/raised bodies and resource-prohibited artificial corpses cannot fund another physical allocation. Known failure is rejected before commitment; a hidden later failure spends the real spell costs without selecting another body. Retain all body/gear/anatomy/history and actual documented needs. New animation HP does not repair limbs, remove Wounds, add proficiencies or duplicate armor.

NEW permanent physical conversion is completed once. The consumed body persists independently of its original caster and its original corpse never becomes fresh fuel again. This is a mechanical body record, not a soul claim or forced cosmological resurrection. Its base HP is initialized once with only genuinely qualifying creation modifiers. Active source-owner/controller changes use actual MAXIMUM_HP rules, not new HP resets. Each is an HP-only animation: ordinary actual0HP destruction leaves consumed remains and real gear; qualifying Null instead preserves the same NullDisabled0 body under the explicit named animation extension, with no Wounds/death-save/Stable engine added. A later genuine resurrection must use its own actual requirements and cannot spend the same allocation twice.

NEW separate ongoing rank-six authority relation: the caster controls at most two such creations across all four alternatives. A successful third creation releases only the predeclared old authority, not that old physical body. Failed creation releases nothing. Creation's initially cooperative mechanical chassis is not a hostile preexisting target and does not claim Command Undeath's initial hard-control admission; any later hostile control must use its own actual save/guard. One actual owner command/offense per numbered round still applies across all assets, and no voluntarily acting creation appears in its creation round. While controlled, use the original owner's actual attached phase. On authority loss/suppression/reacquisition use the explicit finite, no-duplicate schedule transition in CL10-ORDER-01.

Dispelling or suppressing the authority does not undo permanent flesh, destroy the old body, refill HP or create a new corpse. NEW Ghast-only native-action completion — CL10-GHAST-CONE-04
Use a finite axial circular cone measured from the Ghast's actual intact oral outlet, in a declared unit direction. This is NEW Ghast-only geometry, not inherited DI7 geometry and not a new universal cone rule. For a point p, outlet o and unit forward f, let s=(p-o) dot f and r2=|p-o|^2-s^2. The volume has 0<s<=15 feet and r2<=(s/2)^2; its terminal diameter is 15 feet. The actual outlet pose and direction are fixed for this Exhale resolution, not shifted per recipient.
A recipient must have actual body interior intersecting the cone and a clear line of effect from this same outlet to a point in that intersection. Mere touching at an apex, edge or shared exterior face is not body-interior occupancy. Resolve each actual creature once, including an ally or the Ghast if its own actual anatomy truly intersects the forward volume. This is not a perceived-enemy filter and supplies no hidden-recipient announcement, remote sense or public RNG/disclosure adapter. Solid geometry is tested on the actual segment; range and line of effect cannot use different favorable witnesses.
Retain the current profile's choice of Exhale instead of melee: one paid owner command and one shared offensive allowance. Exhale remains a magical nonspell native Action, with one shared 2d6 poison pool, actual creator DC for each CON save, full damage on failure and floored half on success. There is no lingering cloud, Poisoned condition, harvestable toxin, extra RP charge or additional attack. Missing/nonfunctional head or oral route defeats the native action's prerequisites. Current compatible Padded armor, actual AC12, missing left hand and nonproficiency remain as supplied; this CON-save native action is not a STR/DEX attack or spell cast.
In IN6-11-A, outlet (12.5,0,5) and forward +x give s=5.5 and r=1 at the two actual witnesses (18,-1,5) and (18,1,5); 1<=2.75. Both witnesses lie inside their distinct 5-by-5-by-6 bodies. The existing shared faces 3,4 total7; CON17 vs16 takes3, CON7 fails for7. No new dice or secondary rider is introduced.

### completeHistoricalRecordUnchanged

#### spellId

create-undead

#### procedure

KEEP Action, 60 feet, V/S/F, instantaneous creation and all four named alternatives: Ghoul, Ghast, Wight and Mummy. NEW choose exactly one of those four profiles and one distinct perceived Small or Medium Humanoid corpse within range/line of effect. The real corpse must not be summoned, copied, illusory, previously raised, already consumed or a severed fragment; it must retain a substantially continuous torso/head and a legal whole-body space. No arbitrary 24-hour age gate is added: actual decay, missing anatomy and remaining matter determine whether the required body exists. Do not repair harvested/destroyed organs, invent missing limbs or consume a living identity. A hidden ineligible body causes paid failure without a replacement selection. An independently legal resurrection completed before this conversion makes the body living and unavailable.

At resolution consume that physical corpse allocation once and create the chosen finite animation in its original real space. The four explicitly NEW complete profiles are printed immediately below and stored as structured records, rather than substituting a generic zombie. The current 67-record bestiary candidate contains no exact profile for these names; these are proposed spell-created profiles, not silently imported external monster statistics. All have mechanical type Undead, the corpse's actual Small/Medium size, physical mass and surviving anatomy, ordinary sight/hearing and darkvision 60, and no automatic type-derived resistances or immunities. Base HP/AC, six ability scores, permitted movement and Actions are separately supplied for each. Saving throws use original creator's current PB; other checks use its printed ability modifier without proficiency. Actual source-qualified maximum-HP modifiers apply. Attack criticals follow ordinary rules. Genuine usable worn armor may replace base AC under its own rules without creating a second formula or proficiency. Real carried/worn gear retains its prior damage, charges and actual inventory; equipping a new weapon does not silently add an unprinted attack profile.

NEW conversion itself is instantaneous and permanent: later dispelling/antimagic does not recreate the former corpse, undo physical damage or restore the former person. Creator command authority is a separate ongoing rank-six control relation until dispelled/released, with at most two active Create Undead control relations per creator across all four profiles. Before casting at full capacity, declare which existing relation a successfully created new body will release. Failed creation leaves that old relation intact. Creation never destroys a released body to make room. Newborn authority is part of this explicitly created animation chassis, not a hostile control attempt against a living target; it grants no control over a surviving original person. A later hostile control spell still needs its own actual hard-control admission.

While that authority operates, use the shared created-companion command, profile and original-owner statistics. NEW no voluntary movement, Dodge, command, attack or support during its creation numbered round; outside initiative its first potential opportunity is the next ordinary six-second cycle. Passive defenses and conditions still operate. No automatic Wizard standard-minion slot, Corpse Harvest, Auto-Raise, Ghoul Lash, elite identity, class repertoire or monster harvest recipe is gained. These two spell-control slots are separate from genuine other class slots, but all owned bodies retain the one shared command and offensive allowance. Healing can restore its legal current HP without repairing anatomy, curing Wounds, refreshing body provenance or creating resources.

This is an HP-only animated creature: at zero HP it is destroyed without a new dying/death-save/Wound track and leaves its same consumed physical remains and real surviving gear. Actual existing injuries and anatomy restrictions remain through creation and healing. Dispel of creator authority, voluntary release or an admitted slot replacement leaves the living animation with its real profile and present HP. Suppression releases only that relation's control while suppressed; it does not erase the permanent body. A released/suppressed animation has ordinary Director-controlled agency based on actual events, no guaranteed loyalty or automatic hostility, and only its finite profile/basic actions. It regains an independent regular opportunity under the no-duplicate-cycle transition, not an immediate free turn; a suppressed relation resumes with all spent budgets/history retained. Original creation source statistics remain the stated original-owner formulas even after release, not a free new owner's level. Caster incapacity/death alone ends neither body nor relation; unavailable phases give no invented commands. Actual rest may meet genuine needs but supplies no unprinted class/resource recovery. No repeated creator binding ritual is granted by recasting on the now-living animation. Its CR remains unrated: none of these new profiles qualifies for Command Undeath's exact CR 2 predicate without a separate actual CR decision.

Keep original corpse, conversion, animation, current controller, injuries, material loss and inventory events in one physical-provenance ledger. The original body is not simultaneously available for resurrection, harvesting or another animation. A genuine resurrection source must meet its own consumed-body reconstruction requirements; this spell supplies none and decides no soul ownership or destination. The four profiles' varied movement and attack roles, permanent released bodies, two-control cap and unrated CR are consequential new designs for actual GPT review.

#### workedCases

IN6-11-A: A legal corpse becomes one Ghast, not several generic zombies. Its base HP is 50 before actual qualified modifiers, its real missing hand remains missing, and its real armor is neither repaired nor duplicated. It cannot act in the creation numbered round. On a later lawful command it chooses its 15-foot exhalation instead of its melee attack, spending the same shared offensive allowance; the cone can affect allies.

IN6-11-B: A creator already controlling a Ghoul and a Wight successfully creates a Mummy and releases the declared Ghoul relation. The Ghoul remains a real animation at its existing HP and gains no immediate bonus turn or refreshed corpse status. The Mummy uses base HP 70/AC 16 and its distinct strike, subject to real modifiers. Neither released Ghoul nor Mummy gains CR 2 merely because Command Undeath requires that rating.

#### newChoices

NEW one eligible corpse/one distinct named profile, four complete source-transparent designs, permanent conversion separated from two-slot dispellable authority, release agency, no creation-round actions and no invented CR.

### completeSubmittedProcedureUnchanged

Create Homunculus remains Universal rank6,Action,base13RP,V/S/F,60feet and Instantaneous creation. NEW create one physical Tiny mechanical Construct in a currently perceived fitting unoccupied whole-body space within range/LoE. No blood, gem, soul, recipe, consumed material or monetary component is printed or added. Its created body and authority persist without concentration until this source's actual ending. One live body per originating caster: successful replacement ends the old body/control and settles actual carried belongings at the old body's current real location; a countered or failed replacement does not end the old body or refund a paid cast. Physical-body persistence is not a source of harvestable construct parts.

Complete proposed profile: Tiny2.5x2.5 occupied space, actual measured height, body mass8lb, baseHP20 AC14; STR6 DEX14 CON12 INT6 WIS10 CHA6; walk20, Fly30 with Hover, ordinary sight/hearing and Darkvision30. Saves use original creating class-owner currentPB as total; untrained checks use actual listed modifiers. It has small manipulating hands but no skill/tool proficiency, autonomous offense, attack, Multiattack, independent Reaction, spellcasting, class resource pool or copied caster mind. Poison-damage/Poisoned immunity is expressly NEW for this one proposed profile; no other physiology/resistance/immunity follows from Construct.

CHANGE to maximum-HP text: Universal school alone neither grants nor defeats Wizard Conjuration's actual creation bonus. If a Wizard with the genuinely acquired Conjuration feature casts this Wizard-accessible ordinary spell and actually creates this creature, the inherited +Wizard-level maximum HP snapshot applies. A Wizard11 qualifying creation therefore has base20+11=31 before any other genuinely applicable named rule. A non-Conjuration Wizard or another class does not receive it merely from the narrative. The complete current source/access row remains Universal; do not relabel the spell Conjuration or add Wizard access to another class. Flesh Ascendant's Undead predicate does not match this Construct. Later healing/repair does not recreate or refresh that snapshot.

Physical cargo cap is Tiny STR6×7.5=45lb AND3Load, subject to actual hands, fit and containers. Its own8lbbody is not its own cargo but matters to another carrier. At2Load it exceeds half of3 and is Burdened: walk20 becomes10 and Fly30 becomes20, with the actual climbing/swimming/silent-movement penalties when relevant. Combining two2Load parcels still gives4Load and is illegal; calling them one bundle does not erase contents. It cannot transport another creature through this task profile. True carried lantern fuel, damage, ownership and loading time persist.

The creator must issue real simple spoken commands through actual hearing within60feet, with owner perception/LoE and same-plane relation. No telepathy, sensory sharing, hidden knowledge or copied professional judgment is added. In combat a genuinely available Bonus Action directs one simple object-task Action and actual movement in the attached phase, sharing command allowance across assets. A real creation-turn command is allowed but casting supplies none. Fetching a reachable unattended parcel, opening an unlocked physically operable ordinary door, holding a light or sorting known mundane objects are allowed. It cannot attack, activate combat/weapon/healing/spell consumables or dangerous attack machinery, supply Help/flanking, perform skilled judgments or provide missing expertise. A capable uncommanded body only waits/moves/Dodges defensively.

Outside initiative the creator can give one clear safe unskilled standing chore through that same actual channel. The servant performs at most one mundane task interaction per6elapsedseconds plus movement at its actual current Speed, only while the real60-foot supervised relation remains. A new chore requires a new genuine instruction. When supervision/channel ceases it finishes no new unsupervised productive step and waits/defends; there is no remote shop or automatic work Watch. The saved owner's manipulation time does not create crafted output, skill/proficiency, credential, trained assistant, crew attendance, wages, simultaneous job or Expedition office. Apply each actual EX58/crafting task's own real roles and clocks. Actual independent credentials would need their own separate source and are not manufactured by this spell.

NEW maintenance: original creator, one actual Action, touch,2actualRP restores2d6 normalHP to the same still-existing damaged Homunculus up to effective maximum. This is a paid nonspell maintenance operation, not a new spell cast, Counterspell target, Refrain reset, ordinary organic Healing Rite or destroyed-body reconstruction. The2RP is actually paid under this operation, not a spell base price for Nexus/Grand Reservoir or a generic cost waiver; only an exact source expressly applying to this operation could change it. Its2d6 are real repair dice but gain no new presentation/Fate entry here. Broad gain/repair restrictions and actual eligible modifiers still apply; do not assume an organic-healing modifier targets repair. Other repairs need their own exact Construct/body eligibility. Mending explicitly does not refill this HP ledger.

At ordinary0HP destruction it ends without corpse/XP/loot/salvage. Qualifying Null instead leaves the same body NullDisabled0; the explicit maintenance may restore that extant damaged body at0, but can never repair an actually ended one. Full sustaining-source suppression, actual eligible rank-six removal, a successful replacement or creator dismissal ends it and settles real cargo at its actual position. Dismissal is this spell's actual creator Action with perception within60feet/LoE, not the other summons' no-Action concentration release. Caster death/incapacity alone does not invent an ending; absent lawful owner phase/channel there are no new commands. No repair, return, temporary controller or appearance change renews source identity, tasks, HP pool, cargo or permissions.

### completeHistoricalRecordUnchanged

#### spellId

create-homunculus

#### procedure

KEEP creating a servant that carries out commands with Action,60feet,V/S/F. NEW create one physical Tiny Construct in a perceived unoccupied whole-body space within60feet/line of effect. No blood,soul,gem,recipe ingredient or unprinted monetary component is required. Instantaneous is the creation event;the proposed magical body/control persists without concentration until an actual ending below. It is not a permanent supply of ordinary craft matter. One active homunculus per originating caster;successful creation of a replacement ends the old body/control after dropping its real external belongings safely. Countered/failed creation does not replace the old body or refund costs.

NEW complete profile:baseHP20,AC14,body mass8lb,STR6,DEX14,CON12,INT6,WIS10,CHA6;walk20feet,Fly30feet with Hover,ordinary sight/hearing and Darkvision30feet. All six saves use original creating source-owner currentPB as the total modifier;ordinary untrained checks use the listed ability modifier. It has no skill/tool proficiency,spellcasting,class levels,resource pool,attack,Multiattack,Reaction or autonomous offensive Action. Poison-damage and Poisoned immunity follow this expressly proposed profile;no other resistance/condition immunity is implied by Construct. It is a physical body with real Tiny2.5-foot space and actual compatible condition/geometry rules,not Unseen Servant's nonphysical force. Its small hands can manipulate objects actually sized and positioned for them.

Tiny physical carrying capacity is STR6times7.5=45lb and Inventory Load capacity3;both limits apply. Its own8lbbody mass is not its own cargo,but is counted by another real carrier. Simple worn harnesses/containers require actual fit and remain real separate items. It can fetch/carry unattended accessible objects,open an unlocked ordinary door it can physically operate,hold a light,sort known mundane items or perform comparable unskilled safe chores. It cannot carry another creature,read hidden minds,make independent skilled judgments,activate weapons/traps/bombs/poisons/healing or spell items,administer combat consumables,operate a dangerous machinery attack,grant Help/flanking,provide a missing artisan credential or create automatic valuable output/wages. Ordinary physical carrying remains useful;it does not waive loading,ownership,tools,travel hazards or real elapsed time.

The homunculus understands its creator's simple actual spoken commands while able to hear within60feet and with owner perception/line of effect. It supplies no telepathy,sense-sharing,remote report of unknown events or copied personal memories. In combat,a real available Bonus Action directs at most one simple object-task Action plus movement during the attached phase after the whole owner turn,sharing the ordinary companion command ledger. It may receive such a genuine available command in its creation round;creation itself grants none. No command gives an independent Reaction or converts a chore into an attack. Uncommanded capable bodies use their real defensive Move/Dodge behavior and protect their own existence rather than inventing skilled/offensive plans.

Outside combat,the creator may issue one clear unskilled standing chore through the same actual command channel. It performs that chore using its actual movement and at most one mundane task interaction per six elapsed seconds while remaining within the60-foot supervised command relation;changing the chore needs a new actual instruction. This saves the creator's physical manipulation,not the actual time,capacity or qualification of a craft/settlement work order. It is not a trained assistant,an expedition office-holder,a replacement for required active crew attendance or a new independent NPC roster member. When the creator cannot supervise/command,it waits or acts defensively;it cannot run a shop or earn a work Watch on an absent owner's behalf.

Use a single current/maxHP ledger with actual source-qualified bonuses. Its Universal spell origin does not satisfy a Conjuration-only specialization merely because the narrative creates a body. It is an explicitly no-resource-created companion:at0HP it ends,leaving no corpse,experience/harvest reward,free construct parts or copied equipment;actual external carried items drop at their real location under ordinary placement. It has no dying/death-save/Wound/rest-recovery chassis unless an actual future source explicitly supplies one. Ordinary organic healing does not repair this proposed Construct. NEW maintenance option:its original caster may spend an Action,touch and2actualRP to restore2d6HP to a still-existing damaged homunculus,up to its actual maximum. This is a paid maintenance operation,not another spell cast/counter window or free RP/Dissonance reset,and does not reconstruct a destroyed body. Other actual repair effects require their own explicit eligible target interface.

The creator may dismiss it with an Action while perceiving it within60feet/line of effect. Actual eligible rank-six dispel or full sustaining-source suppression ends the body,an explicit NEW lifecycle choice;narrow suppression of one rider follows that narrower scope. No appearance/repair/return resets its held items,HP,spent tasks or control accounting. Caster death/incapacitation alone does not invent a new body ending,but without an actual legal owner phase/channel it receives no new orders. A genuine later control effect uses its own actual authority and budgets without recreating the body or releasing the original one-body reservation. Permanence supplies no soul-origin,resurrection or biological-clone claim.

#### workedCases

TU6-12-A: The Tiny STR6servant has45lbphysical carrying capacity and3Load. A20lb,2Loadpackage can fit both limits in a real compatible carrying arrangement;another2Loadpackage cannot be hidden by calling the two one bundle. In combat the owner's actual Bonus Action directs one fetch interaction,not a second companion attack or an independent skilled Help Action.

TU6-12-B: A homunculus at7/20HPreceives its creator's paid2RPtouch maintenance and2d6=8repair,reaching15/20. Destroying it later at0ends the magical body and drops its actual carried lantern;the lantern's fuel and damage are unchanged. A new13RPcreation can make a new body,but neither repair nor replacement creates free construct salvage or restores the old body's cargo.

#### newChoices

NEW permanent one-body Tiny Construct servant,complete physical/task profile,shared commands,finite paid repair and no-resource ending without invented materials or soul lore.

#### questionId

TU6-12

#### originalQuestion

Preserve creating a SERVANT TO CARRY OUT COMMANDS,Core Action/60ft/Instantaneous. Define complete finite physical profile,size,HP/AC,speeds,senses,abilities,capacity,actions and any material/creation prerequisites as explicit source bindings or NEW additions. Decide permanence,control count,creation-round timing,command costs,repair/reconstruction and ending/suppression. Compare reviewed Unseen Servant and creature/device lifecycle rules without silently choosing whichever offers more benefits. No innate spellcasting,unlimited skilled labor,independent combat turns,copy of the caster's mind or sellable free creature parts follows. Preserve actual body/material ownership and no-resource lineage where justified; the name alone supplies no soul or cosmological origin.

### originalQuestionUnchanged

#### id

CL10-C03-Q01

#### scope

Soulburn Pact / Grand Reservoir

#### question

Does Grand Reservoir’s cannot-combine-with-another-RP-refund-feature restriction prohibit Soulburn’s own0..3 recovery on that waived cast? The supplied zero-recovery waiver comparator avoids silently granting a profitable combination. Please supply the exact positive/blocked ruling before we claim a positive waived-recovery case.

#### status

UNSENT_NEW_TEXT_INTERPRETATION

### disposition

AGREE_WITH_SCOPED_AUTHORSHIP

### decision

Permit only the selected Soulburn spell's intrinsic 0..3 recovery on an actual Grand Reservoir casting under explicit CL10-SOULBURN-RESERVOIR-04. No additional refund feature is authorized.

### reason

This is a new local interface interpretation/exception, not a claim the Wizard card explicitly grants it. It preserves the source-effect recovery and real finite waiver/20-HP cost without globally redefining refund. The zero-output comparator remains valid; a positive-output branch must now cite this proposal.

### disposition

AGREE_WITH_SCOPED_AUTHORSHIP

### decision

Select two usable ordinary gripping extremities for the Wraith, with no new attack or skilled-task permission.

### reason

The source supplies neither hands nor a generic living-ally healing predicate. Ordinary handling has to be authored honestly and still pay actual command/interaction costs; it cannot move real cargo through the wall.

CL10-WRAITH-HANDLING-04

#### scope

Soul Wraith physical weight

#### question

Current geometry fixtures explicitly use0lb for the generated manifestation but do not assert that value is printed or grant carrying immunity. Choose an explicit bounded profile mass (or leave weight-dependent operations conditional) before treating physical-load cases as fully qualified. STR6/Medium ordinary cargo capacities remain independently sourced.

#### status

UNSENT_PROFILE_COMPLETION

### disposition

AGREE_WITH_SCOPED_AUTHORSHIP

### decision

Select 0 lb for this generated Wraith body as an explicit local profile value; Medium space and body Load remain real.

### reason

No printed weight is recovered by this choice. Retaining 0 as an authored pounds input avoids changing existing numeric fixtures while requiring the independently inherited Medium casualty Load and all actual cargo limits. It grants no physics or origin conclusion.

#### id

CL10-C03-Q05

#### scope

Command Undeath / bestiary content

#### question

The frozen26-record bestiary has no full operative authoritative exactCR2 Undead. Both original positive Command cases remain conditional. Confirm the next content-authoring boundary: develop a distinct source-based complete NPC profile in a separate review, or keep these two positive cases open. Do not assignCR2 to an unrated Create Undead body merely to satisfy the tests.

#### status

CONTENT_PREREQUISITE_NOT_AN_ADOPTED_PROFILE

### disposition

AGREE

### decision

Retain both positive Command Undeath whole-case holds; commission a separate complete native-NPC source/profile conference before replacing them with a positive encounter.

### reason

The frozen bestiary remains a source-only 26-record candidate and supplies no operative authoritative exact-CR2 Undead. Create Undead's four authored profiles are unrated and cannot be assigned CR2 merely to meet this admission gate. This is missing content, not permission to change the spell to CR<=2 or to invent an encounter.

##### procedure

KEEP Action,one perceived creature60feet,4d8necrotic per round and heal half. NEW reconcile Core Instantaneous with a concentration channel lasting at most60seconds, with one initial pulse during the casting Action and at most nine further pulses, never more than ten total. The initial pulse makes a ranged spell attack against the declared creature using the real casting source; hit deals4d8necrotic (ordinary attack critical applies), miss deals none. Only one original target exists; no retarget. At each future regular caster turn an Action may maintain one further pulse against that same still-existing original creature target, with perception/range60/line of effect rechecked. At most one pulse per original regular caster-turn cycle; extra turns/Actions grant none. If no maintenance Action is completed during an available future regular turn, the channel ends at that turn's END; a miss still counts as maintenance. Outside initiative an otherwise capable caster spends the equivalent whole Action at each successive six-second cycle; missed maintenance ends it. Loss of concentration, actual caster incapacity, target death, actual separation beyond60feet, loss of line of effect/perception, removal or expiry ends the channel immediately; suppression makes a pulse unavailable, consumes its scheduled opportunity and therefore causes missed-maintenance ending if it spans the entire relevant turn/cycle. No paused beam or stored future pulse exists. A hit's healing resolves after its damage/life consequences, even if that hit killed the target and thereby ended later pulses: caster receives ordinary base healing floor(actualDamageDealt/2), using this pulse's eligible terminal branches once and excluding duplicated parents, overkill and copied/replayed descendants. Do not halve nominal dice before counting actual damage or require ordinary HP loss when tempHP was truly absorbed. Damage transfer remains one root; sum its actual terminal recipients without healing separately for each. Apply genuine ordinary healing modifiers, broad prohibition, current maximum/Critical ceiling and source causal guards. Full casterHP, blocked healing or a miss stores nothing and refunds nothing. Unlike Lifedraw's expressly narrower profile, this proposal adds no living-nonconstruct/allied/helpless exclusion to the printed damage and heal clause; necrotic defenses still govern actual numbers. Damage/HP cannot be farmed from an invented summon resource pool or overkill. A subsequent paid cast begins a genuinely new channel and concentration, not a free same-channel extra pulse; all real Action/RP/Dissonance costs apply. The proposal's ten-pulse ceiling and continuous maintenance are NEW balance choices for joint review.

CURRENT HEALING-ELIGIBILITY COMPLETION: Exclude any terminal branch whose actual transfer/source flags forbid derived healing or recovery before summing the base-healing pool. The original NE5-06-B arithmetic is conditional on a genuinely permitted split, not a claim that Burden Shared or Life Linked allows it. The two supplied source-qualified vectors show their actual narrower results and retain the original abstract scenario unchanged. Neither vector changes the separate pending broad donor predicate for this spell.

CURRENT TARGET-CONTINUITY COMPLETION — Enervation only: "Still-existing original creature" is the target identity/lifecycle predicate, not a new living-anatomy or nonconstruct restriction. A continuing active creature classified as nonliving is not excluded solely by that classification. Its actual defenses, damage/source eligibility and no-resource/no-recovery flags still apply to each packet and any healing funding. A corpse, removed body, ended summon or destroyed entity that no longer qualifies as a creature cannot supply a maintained target. A same identity at 0 HP is tested under its actual continuing-creature/Disabled/death state, not assumed dead or alive from the HP number. The attack that actually ends the target finishes its own causal damage and source-qualified healing before future channel opportunities are canceled. This completion does not import Leeching Plague's hostile-living-nonconstruct donor filter, provide healing to a dead caster, or grant repair, resurrection, resource farming or an exception to another source's exclusion flags.

###### newChoices

NEW one-target ranged attack channel, concentration60seconds/ten pulses, real future regular-turn maintenance Actions and continuous range/perception tether. Healing uses reviewed actualDamageDealt terminal accounting, without silently extending Lifedraw-specific exclusions.

###### workedCases

NE5-06-A: A hit rolls18necrotic against an unresistant creature with5tempHP and8normalHP. It absorbs5tempHP and loses8normalHP, so actualDamageDealt is13and base healing6; the remaining5overkill contributes nothing. If the actual death ends the channel, this already-resolved pulse can still supply its6base healing once.

NE5-06-B: A14damage pulse is legally divided into two terminal branches that actually inflict4and6. Sum10and heal base5once, not half14plus both children. At fullHP the caster restores0and banks nothing. An extra turn grants no second maintenance pulse; missing the next regular turn's Action ends the channel.

###### questionId

NE5-06

###### originalQuestion

Preserve4d8 NECROTIC EACH ROUND and healing for HALF, Core Action/60ft/Instantaneous. Define one target or another explicit finite scope,initial event and repeated checkpoints,lifetime/concentration,attack/save/outcomes,maintenance actions and range/line requirements. Bind healing to one actual final paid HP-damage event with current rounding; distinguish tempHP,overkill,damage resistance/immunity,transfer,absorbed damage and simultaneous recipients. Identify who heals and how healing prevention/full HP or target death ends or consumes the opportunity. Compare reviewed Vampiric Touch/Lifedraw and damage sharing, without borrowing their attack or duration wholesale. No healing from nominal dice,duplicate shared/copy packets or banked missed ticks; recasts,suppression and extra turns cannot create extra rounds.
