##### previousSharedProposalUnchanged

###### status

UNADOPTED AND UNSENT. Sixteen complete local procedures and thirty-two authored examples await actual GPT discussion and exact review. NEW denotes an explicit design proposal. Sources, catalog access, reviewed status, runtime and cosmology remain unchanged.

###### casting

KEEP actual rank-five school access, minimum class levels and base10RP. All sixteen keep Action and range60feet except Mass Decay keeps30feet. All keep V/S/F except Danse Macabre keeps V/S/M/F and the reviewed ordinary material/focus alternative; no unprinted consumed material price is imported. Actual source casting ability, repertoire, components, permissions, RP/strain surcharges, Dissonance/Refrain and countering apply. DC=8+actual PB+actual casting modifier; spell attack=actual PB+actual casting modifier. A legal commitment pays actual costs before hidden target checks and countering, without refunds. Known illegal targets/costs are rejected at preflight. Only explicitly proposed concentration requirements apply; new concentration ends the prior one at its actual commitment, even if the new cast fails. No automatic upcasting, class feature or outside statblock is acquired.

###### geometry

Named targets and chosen centers require actual perception and caster line of effect. Areas also require center-to-recipient line of effect; an area alone does not require seeing every indiscriminate recipient. A designated enemy is a perceived creature the caster regards as hostile from current information, not a hidden alignment detector. Choose ordered lists, modes, limbs, shapes and paths before resolution and hidden results. Recheck eligibility/geometry at resolution; hidden failure creates no diagnostic. Actual movement and body volumes cannot pass through walls or other solid/occupied spaces. Forced movement spends no movement budget and causes no ordinary opportunity attack; actual loss of support uses the reviewed Fall Marker, not a second damage event invented by this packet.

###### clocks

A proposed minute is60 elapsed seconds, hour3600 and day86400. Expiry wins at its exact deadline. Suppression never resets expiry, used payments, per-round history or recovery history; skip suppressed opportunities without storing them. Genuine extra turns do not advance regular START/END counters or grant another explicitly regular-turn pulse. For the three-round spell endpoint, record the next three future original-source regular END markers, excluding a turn already underway; outside initiative use18elapsed seconds. Preserve that original schedule through death, initiative changes and transition out of combat, with six-second virtual cycles when the source lacks an active turn. This is a NEW finite clock bridge, not permission to halt a spell by removing its caster from initiative.

###### damageAndHealing

Use reviewed typed defenses, minimum-HP prevention, temporary HP, Wounds, Instant Death, dying/Stable, actual death and eligible immediate survival responses. Resistance halves the matching component with normal rounding. actualDamageDealt=tempHP absorbed+actual ordinary HP lost, excluding overkill, state-assignment loss and double-counted parent/child transfer packets. Count each eligible terminal damage branch once. Healing based on this number remains ordinary healing unless a procedure explicitly identifies direct state recovery. Broad cannot-regain-HP applies to living direct state recovery; ordinary healing multipliers and Critical ordinary-healing ceilings do not automatically apply to it. HP costs use normal HP only, are not damage and cannot be paid with tempHP, redirected, reduced, prevented or refunded by a failed benefit. No spell here revives an actually dead identity.

###### companions

All commanded bodies of one owner share the reviewed one Bonus Action command and one offensive companion Action per numbered round, including other class companions/devices. Issue within60feet with actual owner perception and line of effect. NEW these spell-created/animated undead understand only their owner's simple mental command channel; it grants neither a remote sensory feed nor free speech/knowledge. A BA selects exactly one body and one ordinary Move plus its single profiled Action or ordinary permitted basic Action, executed only in the owner's existing attached phase after the whole owner turn. Every other capable body has only defensive Move and Dodge in that phase. No independent initiative, Reaction, BA, multiattack, spellcasting, Help engine, tool proficiency, resource pool or summon power exists. Committed orders retain reviewed validity rules. Damage/control consumes the shared offensive allowance; support still needs its paid command. Original source owner supplies the current spell attack and PB-based saves; changing controller grants no second cap or new source stats. Each independent application keeps its actual duration and original ownership. Legal ordinary companion healing cannot reconstruct a destroyed body or refill absent resources.

###### bodyClaims

One physical identity has one state: living, corpse, consumed or removed. Animate only actual eligible non-summoned, non-copied, non-illusory, not-previously-raised remains; fragments do not become extra bodies. Legal resurrection already completed during immediate death responses marks that same body living before later claims. A reservation by Auto-Raise is not exclusive ownership; still resolve its actual later checkpoint and capacity, consuming no second body. These spells do not grant Wizard Corpse Harvest, Auto-Raise, Infernal Pact or Mage assets. Spell-specific body caps are separate from Wizard's three standard-minion slots and Mage asset slots, but all retain the shared command/offense budget. NEW spent animated remains retain their physical identity and actual inventory, with consumed status: no second raising/harvest fuel and no refreshed recipe donor qualities. An actual resurrection source must meet its reviewed reconstruction requirements for a consumed body; this packet grants none. Created summons leave no corpse/loot. Source-qualified maximum-HP modifiers require genuine access and actual spell provenance; a Wizard spell creation can qualify for its actual Conjuration modifier, never a generic borrowed Wizard level. Printed HP here is base HP before such genuinely applicable modifiers.

###### zombieProfile

NEW shared Flood/Danse zombie animation profile: actual Small or Medium eligible body, type Undead; baseAC10, baseHP20, walk20feet only with its original functional walking supports; STR12, DEX6, CON14, INT3, WIS6, CHA3. All six saving throws use original owner's current PB as their total modifier; other checks use the listed ability modifier with no proficiency. Ordinary sight/hearing only, no darkvision, languages, private memory or remote senses. It makes one melee attack at5feet using original owner's current spell-attack modifier, dealing1d6 bludgeoning with its actual available arm/body strike, no ability damage bonus. Native shape/size/weight and missing anatomy remain real: no hand attack without a surviving striking limb; missing legs give no new crawl/flight/swim mode. Physical wounds/injuries and harvest losses persist and restrict the surviving anatomy; the new animation HP pool does not repair them. Existing usable worn armor can replace the baseAC under its own rules without creating proficiency, a new item or a second AC formula. Supplied equipment stays real, encumbers normally, and does not add weapon attacks outside this profile. No resistances, immunities, Undead Fortitude, regeneration, death saves, loot reward, donor substrate or other zombie powers are imported. This is an HP-only animation destroyed at0; destruction or expiry leaves that same consumed inert body and its real surviving gear. It neither restores nor duplicates the former living identity.

###### materialLedger

NEW permanent material conversions below are explicit finite transmutation exceptions: output occupies the same selected eligible volume, while its mass becomes that of its new named mundane material. They do not claim both mass and volume conservation. Record each actual source region/lot, volume, old and new composition/mass, contamination, claims, damage and later splits/removals. No original stock remains separately available. No new source-tier, precious mineral, monster property, finished item quality, magical reagent role or trade XP follows from renaming matter. Separating ordinary output does not copy the original region; consumed/sold quantities are gone. Existing tools, actual work, access, Load, transport, finite buyers, provenance and recipe requirements still apply. Transformation is not Fabricate, mining work, an appraisal or a guaranteed sale. A permanent instantaneous result is ordinary matter: later Dispel/suppression does not revert it. Real later physical melting, erosion and use change that actual stock once. Source contamination/embedded foreign matter is not purified by changing its host material. No cosmological origin or universal matter-creation rule follows.

###### examples

The32 cases are authored adjudications, not full runtime tests. Unless stated otherwise, caster is Wizard9 with the actual Necromancy/Transmutation repertoire row, PB4, INT+4, maximum/currentRP32, Dissonance0 and a different preceding spell. An otherwise unmodified cast costs10RP (32→22), raises Dissonance to1 with limit10, uses attack+8 and DC16. Scenario-specific immunity, anatomy, positions, HP and other features are explicit premises, not new grants.

Actual regular Action maintenance,10pulse ceiling, continuous60ft/perception tether and missing-turn deadline; no extra-turn pulse; actual terminal branch heal and differently scoped donor predicate vs Leeching Plague; named transfer source.

#### completeSharedRules

##### P20-CASTING-01

Reuse actual class-access/repertoire, paid costs, component, concentration and Dissonance/Refrain procedures from the exact registry. None of these drafts grants a new class row, rank, upcast or resource maximum. A continuous field, stored rider or command does not itself cast again. Actual target/caster predicates are rechecked at the source-defined checkpoint, without refund or diagnostic disclosure from hidden failure.

##### P20-DIGITAL-GEOMETRY-01

NEW bounded clarification for these 20 proposals: measure the actual occupied body against the continuous authored volume/surface, with actual cover/line of effect. Five-foot lengths remain real lengths, not a rounding of positions onto tabletop squares. Source-authored five-foot destructible/fuel partitions remain fixed object identities; changing path sampling, zoom or visual mesh subdivision cannot create more targets, HP or exposure. Evaluate a genuine entry at the first boundary crossing/contact, resolve it once before continuing the same trajectory, and preserve actual remaining movement. Teleport crosses no intermediate path; an arrival can trigger only a source explicitly authorizing arrival. Snapshot simultaneous recipients once before their consequences; destroyed cover creates no second wave.

##### P20-CLOCKS-01

NEW explicit cohort bindings: absolute field deadlines are resolution+60 seconds where the procedure states one minute; expiry wins at equality. Source-owned pulses at resolution and +6 through +54 do not require a caster turn or grant a creature turn. A finite target/source marker schedule records its original regular markers at creation under accepted time ordering; reorder, extra turns and departure preserve the original virtual schedule, six seconds apart. Explicit future-turn endpoints exclude an in-progress turn/phase; an ending save expressly including current END keeps that exception. At START, apply accepted expiry/recovery/death-save ordering before periodic damage. At END, due damage/eligible ending saves precede that source endpoint cleanup, except a card explicitly expires before its redundant last save. Suppression consumes a due scheduled opportunity with no damage or catch-up. Ended creature identities have no recipient; administrative deadline bookkeeping never resurrects one.

##### P20-ATTACHED-PHASE-01

NEW source-specific bridge for the target-clock proposals in this cohort, not a general companion turn: an owner-attached recipient uses the original owner regular attached-phase beginning/end only for the stated spell packet or ending save. A future phase excludes one already underway. If no such real phase exists, retain a six-second spell-only checkpoint and finite original endpoint. This checkpoint supplies only a due packet/save or expiry that the spell actually grants; it supplies no Action, movement, Bonus Action, Reaction refresh, recharge, or ordinary death save. Source-required real Action/command maintenance cannot execute at an administrative checkpoint. Root identity, prior spent exposures, endpoint and finite tick count survive controller or schedule changes.

##### P20-FAMILY-EXPOSURE-01

NEW per-source anti-duplication applies only where the individual procedure names a family cap: record target identity, named spell family, explicit category and numbered round. Different owners/casts share that named ledger; distinct spell names and explicitly separate Wall of Fire categories do not merge. Outside initiative use the scene continuing six-second exposure cycle, fixed before the relevant exposure. Preserve its spent state through a transition to the current combat round until that mapped interval ends; never refresh just by changing timing mode. Immunity/save/prevention after actual admission still consumes the exposure; wholly suppressed or geometrically ineligible sources cannot claim it. Simultaneous eligible sources use established application order then stable identity. Immolation instead uses the already accepted ongoing-fire arbitration at each identical target marker.

##### P20-DAMAGE-FUNDING-01

Reuse actual typed defenses, temporary HP, HP floors, Wounds, concentration, death/Null life order and one original recipient event. No inferred damage amount from structural failure or overkill. ActualDamageDealt remains real tempHP absorption plus normal HP loss. Healing/funding is a separate consumer: first exclude any terminal branch whose actual transfer/source flag forbids derived healing or recovery, then apply the spell-specific donor predicate, sum eligible branches once and floor the half once. Never count a split parent again. Burden Shared and Life Linked restrictions remain operative; a numeric branch amount alone is not funding permission. Preserve the distinct pending Leeching Plague donor exclusions and Enervation wider proposal for explicit Director disposition.

##### P20-RNG-INFORMATION-01

Authored dice are engine RNG. Original component identity, draw method, save factor and cached ordinal value remain private engine state unless an actual separately accepted presentation policy grants the exact disclosure. This candidate grants no new public dice viewer, hidden-target list, successful-save feedback, enemy-identity scan or Fate eligibility. Shared damage dice do not merge recipient roots. The accepted exact DE4 five-entry presentation policy applies only where its actual source predicates pass; it does not automatically disclose any private periodic, delayed, penalty or random-selection operation in this cohort. An actual qualifying OVERT-ATTACK d20 can use that entry without exposing a hidden Synaptic Static d6, hidden modifier cause or later damage pool. No sixth presentation entry is proposed here. No RNG seed or future draw is exposed. If a source has no admitted recipient and no explicit source-authored event-generation rule, do not invent a damage event merely to publish a roll.

##### P20-ROOT-01

Every damage source uses its explicit pending per-source mapping in damageRootProposals. These are NEW decisions requiring concurrence; accepted Forbiddance/Chain Arc/Skybrand and lower nightmare rules do not silently generalize. Root creation alone spends no Action, deals no damage, triggers no Wound, generates no material, and grants no hidden information. Pure transfer retains flags; an attached component can retain an old flag while a genuinely new operated attack creates its own root. Physical fire and Sonic debris mappings are bounded to the exact finite proposed outputs.

##### P20-SUPPRESSION-01

Reuse the exact actual suppressor/removal source. Lesser Antimagic, rank limits and source versus expression tests keep their accepted scope. AC6 Globe is not general antimagic: its fixed boundary blocks only its actual incoming rank-0–5 source cases, does not ban all internal casting, and does not erase an already attached buff merely because a creature enters. A fixed external ongoing field and a target-attached rider have different origin facts. A spell object is not a creature; physical Inferno fuel/rubble after actual creation is not a sustained magical field. No suppression pauses a deadline or restores a destroyed body, cleared crystal patch, spent shot or old command.

##### procedure

Action; range 60 feet; V/S/F. KEEP 2d6 per round for four rounds and healing the caster for half. NEW choose one perceived creature with line of effect; it makes CON. Success establishes no effect. Failure attaches a nonconcentration magical stream with exactly four 2d6 necrotic installments at its next four future regular END markers, excluding an in-progress turn. There is no immediate fifth packet, attack roll, attack critical or repeat ending save. Outside initiative use +6/+12/+18/+24 seconds; use the shared attached-phase bridge where required. Each due installment rolls separately, applies current actual defenses/sharing and consumes its opportunity even if suppressed or reduced to zero. NEW heal the original caster once by floor(eligible actualDamageDealt/2) after that installment’s damage/life consequences finish. Eligible funding is limited to terminal damage received by genuinely hostile living nonconstruct actual encounter opponents; self, allies, controlled donors, helpless captives, corpses and no-resource summoned/copied bodies contribute zero. Those predicates constrain the heal, not whether the initial creature is a legal damage target. Eligible temporary-HP absorption and actual normal-HP loss count; overkill, prevented loss, an unsplit parent counted again and unrelated reflected/counterdamage do not. Sum eligible recipient branches once and then floor half once. The actual caster must still be a continuing living recipient of legal healing; zero HP alone is not death and normal recovery rules apply. A dead/ended caster receives no heal and cannot redirect it to an heir, proxy, replacement body record or companion. If actual absence leaves no valid receiving body under the source, skip that heal without banking it. Ordinary distance and loss of initial sight alone do not end this established nonconcentration stream or create a new recipient. Actual target death/ended identity ends future installments; zero HP alone follows its real life rules. Source death does not cancel already established damage installments, but supplies no post-death heal. Each installment’s secondary healing invokes only independently eligible healing hooks and cannot reproduce the drain or another heal from the same packet. A successful same-caster replacement ends the old remaining schedule; other owners have separate paid streams. Qualifying dispel ends the stream; immunity to Disease or removal of Disease alone does not, because this spell adds no Disease, contagion, healing prohibition or physical pathogen.

CURRENT HEALING-ELIGIBILITY COMPLETION: Before the proposed hostile-living-nonconstruct donor filter, exclude terminal transferred branches marked no-derived-healing or no-recovery by the actual source. Burden Shared and Life Linked cannot fund this secondary healing. A transfer must have actual access, resources, timing and acceptance; do not manufacture two eligible donors from a symbolic split. Preserve the original IN4-08 examples unchanged as history.

###### newChoices

NEW initial CON-negates application, necrotic type, exactly four future-END installments without concentration, explicitly restricted numeric actual-damage heal including eligible buffers, and source/recipient life-state handling. Lifedraw, Vampiric Touch and Soul Tap retain their nonidentical funding rules.

###### workedCases

IN4-08-A: The first installment rolls 9 against a qualifying enemy with 3 temporary HP and 4 normal HP. With no prevention, numeric actual damage is 3+4=7, not the overkill-inclusive 9; the caster’s one base heal is floor(7/2)=3, then actual healing limits apply. If that enemy dies, the three remaining opportunities end.

IN4-08-B: A surviving target receives the stream outside initiative at t=0. Opportunities are t=6,12,18,24. Suppression at t=12 spends that installment without damage/heal; lifting it at t=13 adds no replacement tick. There was no initial t=0 damage, so the maximum remains four installments.

###### questionId

###### originalQuestion

Preserve2d6 per round for four rounds and healing the caster for half, Action/60ft. Define target and save/attack, damage type, first tick and exactly four scheduled opportunities, repeat saves/concentration and ending. Bind healing basis and rounding to an explicit damage quantity under current Lifedraw/Vampiric Touch, defenses, tempHP, damage sharing and healing restrictions. Define zero damage, excess/overkill, dead/absent caster and target loss. Plague alone does not grant contagion or Disease; no fifth initial tick, off-clock extra-turn tick, healing copied by each relay or return of missed ticks after suppression.

##### previousSharedProposalUnchanged

###### status

UNADOPTED AND UNSENT. Thirteen complete proposed mechanical procedures with twenty-six authored examples. NEW completions await actual joint discussion and exact review. No catalog operational field, ordinary approval, class access, runtime, balance or cosmology status changes.

###### casting

KEEP each actual class access row, rank 4/base 7 RP, minimum class level and repertoire. All thirteen use Action and V/S/F; Summon Corpse Golem preserves V/S/M/F with the existing unpriced material-or-focus alternative. The ordinary casting engine, actual hand/voice/focus requirements, RP discounts and surcharges, Dissonance/Refrain and source hooks apply. Observable legality precedes commitment; hidden defenses or concealed ineligibility give no free diagnostic or refund. Commit the actual Action, costs and cadence before the counter window. A concentration cast displaces incompatible old concentration at commitment even if countered. Attack modifier is actual source PB plus casting modifier; save DC is 8 plus those values. Saves are binary, without natural-die degree shifts. No generic upcast, level scaling, extra turn or unacquired source is supplied.

###### clocks

NEW for these cards, durations expressed in minutes/hours use absolute elapsed deadlines from resolution: one minute is sixty seconds and one hour is 3,600 seconds. Expiry precedes a use scheduled at exactly that deadline. A hostile one-round rider uses the first future target regular END excluding the in-progress turn; N future target-end installments use precisely N such endpoints. Outside initiative use the stated 6N seconds. Preserve the chosen virtual marker or absolute deadline through reordering, extra turns, absence, entry and exit from initiative. NEW attached-asset bridge for Mind Maze and Leeching Plague: if the recipient has only a real original-owner attached phase, use its next future attached-phase END excluding a phase already underway; if it has no real turn/phase, use six-second END-only checkpoints. Mapping a checkpoint supplies no ordinary turn, Action, death save, refresh or extra tick. Due effects run before their own ending cleanup. Suppressed scheduled installments are spent without effect; they never accumulate.

###### identityAndMultiplicity

NEW explicit group default: one active instance per original caster per ordinary spell ID; a successfully established replacement ends only that caster’s old active instance. An invalid or countered nonconcentration cast leaves the old instance, while ordinary concentration displacement still happens at commitment. A successfully resisted cast establishes nothing and does not replace an old nonconcentration rider. Different owners keep separate source records and endpoints, with stated same-family caps and the existing strongest same-stat flat-bonus rules. Do not generalize that bonus rule into a universal strongest-effect rule. Each record retains caster, class, rank, cast ID, snapshotted spell DC, recipients/anchor, actual history and ending. Transfer, form change or a new database ID cannot rearm it. Nonconcentration voluntary dismissal here requires the original caster’s Action and perception/line of effect to the recipient or anchor within original range; completed instantaneous consequences cannot be recalled.

###### perceptionAndIllusions

KEEP actual sight, hearing, Hidden, cover and Invisible rules. Ordinary invisibility does not hide location automatically. Once an observer knows location, it loses only Invisible-specific attack advantage/disadvantage; sight-required targeting still requires actual sight. Resolve a revealing attack first, then give location knowledge only to observers perceiving its evidence. Alternate senses work only within their real scope. NEW apply the current LR-G07 examination procedure to these new visual illusions: an Action with a relevant accessible sense permits INT (Investigation) against the creating spell’s snapshotted DC, using Core degrees/natural shifts then collapsing Success/Critical Success versus Failure/Critical Failure. Failure permits another attempt only with genuinely new evidence or method. Conclusive personally witnessed physical contradiction identifies the false part without a roll. A warning enables examination but does not globally identify the spell for everybody. Images add no actual cover, collision, support, light, sound, creature body, remote senses or inventory unless expressly supplied below. No compelled belief, automatic disguise success or knowledge of hidden terrain is granted.

###### damageHealingAndCosts

KEEP typed damage defenses, actual prevention/sharing, buffers, life-state rules, Wounds and concentration. Save-half floors before the relevant defenses. Numeric actualDamageDealt is temporary HP absorbed plus actual normal HP lost, excluding overkill and prevented HP-floor loss. For a permitted drain sum eligible terminal recipient branches once, never both a split parent and its children; apply the card’s narrower funding predicate before its final half/floor. HP restoration uses actual healing restrictions and maximums and grants no automatic Wound cure, resurrection or resource refresh. Direct HP payment is separate from damage: no resistance, buffer, transfer, Wound/damage trigger or damage-derived refund. It must be paid from real normal HP under the card’s explicit floor. No necrotic, shadow, plague or soul name creates an unprinted condition, material, pool or setting fact.

###### assetsAndControl

KEEP the real owner-wide one command and one offensive companion/device allowance per numbered round. Commands use the owner’s Bonus Action on their regular turn, actual audible/visible communication within sixty feet, and execute in the attached phase after that whole turn. No immediate creation movement/attack and no extra-turn refresh. An issued command survives later owner incapacity only while the actual source and other eligibility survive. Uncommanded assets can move defensively and Dodge, subject to conditions. Current owner-source PB supplies saves; specified base HP/AC remain fixed except genuinely acquired source-qualified modifiers. No borrowed monster abilities or unstated condition/damage immunity. For the two NEW summoned bodies below and Bone Cage only, full suppression of that creating source ends that body/structure immediately rather than storing an intangible replacement. Actual riders or narrowly scoped suppression still affect only their own scope. Ending drops actual cargo/riders at their actual locations with ordinary support/Falling; it creates no salvage, corpse, duplicate equipment or reappearance. This explicit NEW ending rule does not amend other summons.

###### hardControlAndMovement

KEEP the source-character/target/encounter hard-denial allowance shared across that owner’s effects. A whole-Action/turn denial spends it only when established; resistance, immunity or an already-used allowance supplies no invented fallback. Soft Speed effects, an obstacle and a numeric attack/save penalty do not themselves spend it. Teleportation relocates without spending Speed, traversing the path or provoking ordinary opportunity attacks. Speed 0 alone is not a relocation prohibition; an actual teleport/relocation prohibition still applies. Verify whole body, actual legal cargo, occupancy and destination before resolution. Only actual carried noncreature belongings travel unless a card expressly permits passengers; another creature, grappled victim, carried living body or mount stays with its own real position/support. No route between planes, custody bypass, resource refill or returned movement budget is implied.

##### sourcePromise

2d6/round four rounds and half heal retained; name does not create Disease.

##### remainingDecisions

Four targetENDs, exact terminal damage/temporaryHP/overkill, proposed hostile living nonconstruct funding predicate needs source decision vs Enervation; caster death keeps damage/noheal; actual targets/sources/transfer fixtures.

#### completeSharedRules

##### P20-CASTING-01

Reuse actual class-access/repertoire, paid costs, component, concentration and Dissonance/Refrain procedures from the exact registry. None of these drafts grants a new class row, rank, upcast or resource maximum. A continuous field, stored rider or command does not itself cast again. Actual target/caster predicates are rechecked at the source-defined checkpoint, without refund or diagnostic disclosure from hidden failure.

##### P20-DIGITAL-GEOMETRY-01

NEW bounded clarification for these 20 proposals: measure the actual occupied body against the continuous authored volume/surface, with actual cover/line of effect. Five-foot lengths remain real lengths, not a rounding of positions onto tabletop squares. Source-authored five-foot destructible/fuel partitions remain fixed object identities; changing path sampling, zoom or visual mesh subdivision cannot create more targets, HP or exposure. Evaluate a genuine entry at the first boundary crossing/contact, resolve it once before continuing the same trajectory, and preserve actual remaining movement. Teleport crosses no intermediate path; an arrival can trigger only a source explicitly authorizing arrival. Snapshot simultaneous recipients once before their consequences; destroyed cover creates no second wave.

##### P20-CLOCKS-01

NEW explicit cohort bindings: absolute field deadlines are resolution+60 seconds where the procedure states one minute; expiry wins at equality. Source-owned pulses at resolution and +6 through +54 do not require a caster turn or grant a creature turn. A finite target/source marker schedule records its original regular markers at creation under accepted time ordering; reorder, extra turns and departure preserve the original virtual schedule, six seconds apart. Explicit future-turn endpoints exclude an in-progress turn/phase; an ending save expressly including current END keeps that exception. At START, apply accepted expiry/recovery/death-save ordering before periodic damage. At END, due damage/eligible ending saves precede that source endpoint cleanup, except a card explicitly expires before its redundant last save. Suppression consumes a due scheduled opportunity with no damage or catch-up. Ended creature identities have no recipient; administrative deadline bookkeeping never resurrects one.

##### P20-ATTACHED-PHASE-01

NEW source-specific bridge for the target-clock proposals in this cohort, not a general companion turn: an owner-attached recipient uses the original owner regular attached-phase beginning/end only for the stated spell packet or ending save. A future phase excludes one already underway. If no such real phase exists, retain a six-second spell-only checkpoint and finite original endpoint. This checkpoint supplies only a due packet/save or expiry that the spell actually grants; it supplies no Action, movement, Bonus Action, Reaction refresh, recharge, or ordinary death save. Source-required real Action/command maintenance cannot execute at an administrative checkpoint. Root identity, prior spent exposures, endpoint and finite tick count survive controller or schedule changes.

##### P20-FAMILY-EXPOSURE-01

NEW per-source anti-duplication applies only where the individual procedure names a family cap: record target identity, named spell family, explicit category and numbered round. Different owners/casts share that named ledger; distinct spell names and explicitly separate Wall of Fire categories do not merge. Outside initiative use the scene continuing six-second exposure cycle, fixed before the relevant exposure. Preserve its spent state through a transition to the current combat round until that mapped interval ends; never refresh just by changing timing mode. Immunity/save/prevention after actual admission still consumes the exposure; wholly suppressed or geometrically ineligible sources cannot claim it. Simultaneous eligible sources use established application order then stable identity. Immolation instead uses the already accepted ongoing-fire arbitration at each identical target marker.

##### P20-DAMAGE-FUNDING-01

Reuse actual typed defenses, temporary HP, HP floors, Wounds, concentration, death/Null life order and one original recipient event. No inferred damage amount from structural failure or overkill. ActualDamageDealt remains real tempHP absorption plus normal HP loss. Healing/funding is a separate consumer: first exclude any terminal branch whose actual transfer/source flag forbids derived healing or recovery, then apply the spell-specific donor predicate, sum eligible branches once and floor the half once. Never count a split parent again. Burden Shared and Life Linked restrictions remain operative; a numeric branch amount alone is not funding permission. Preserve the distinct pending Leeching Plague donor exclusions and Enervation wider proposal for explicit Director disposition.

##### P20-RNG-INFORMATION-01

Authored dice are engine RNG. Original component identity, draw method, save factor and cached ordinal value remain private engine state unless an actual separately accepted presentation policy grants the exact disclosure. This candidate grants no new public dice viewer, hidden-target list, successful-save feedback, enemy-identity scan or Fate eligibility. Shared damage dice do not merge recipient roots. The accepted exact DE4 five-entry presentation policy applies only where its actual source predicates pass; it does not automatically disclose any private periodic, delayed, penalty or random-selection operation in this cohort. An actual qualifying OVERT-ATTACK d20 can use that entry without exposing a hidden Synaptic Static d6, hidden modifier cause or later damage pool. No sixth presentation entry is proposed here. No RNG seed or future draw is exposed. If a source has no admitted recipient and no explicit source-authored event-generation rule, do not invent a damage event merely to publish a roll.

##### P20-ROOT-01

Every damage source uses its explicit pending per-source mapping in damageRootProposals. These are NEW decisions requiring concurrence; accepted Forbiddance/Chain Arc/Skybrand and lower nightmare rules do not silently generalize. Root creation alone spends no Action, deals no damage, triggers no Wound, generates no material, and grants no hidden information. Pure transfer retains flags; an attached component can retain an old flag while a genuinely new operated attack creates its own root. Physical fire and Sonic debris mappings are bounded to the exact finite proposed outputs.

##### P20-SUPPRESSION-01

Reuse the exact actual suppressor/removal source. Lesser Antimagic, rank limits and source versus expression tests keep their accepted scope. AC6 Globe is not general antimagic: its fixed boundary blocks only its actual incoming rank-0–5 source cases, does not ban all internal casting, and does not erase an already attached buff merely because a creature enters. A fixed external ongoing field and a target-attached rider have different origin facts. A spell object is not a creature; physical Inferno fuel/rubble after actual creation is not a sustained magical field. No suppression pauses a deadline or restores a destroyed body, cleared crystal patch, spent shot or old command.
